Conclusion: 石墨烯/h-BN异质结构具有气体传感潜力，其吸附性能强烈依赖于h-BN覆盖层的结构（完全覆盖vs部分覆盖），这为设计高性能气体传感器提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Using periodic DFT, we examined the adsorption of NO2, NH3, and O3 on the h-BN side of a graphene/h-BN heterostructure designed as a model gas sensor material. The h-BN overlayer serves both as an active adsorption surface and as protection that may reduce irreversible processes such as graphene oxidation. Two model systems were considered: an extended graphene/h-BN bilayer (B36N36C72) and a graphene sheet partially covered by a smaller h-BN island (B11N11C72). Their electronic structures differ strongly near the Dirac point. In the extended bilayer, the Fermi level remains aligned with that of pristine graphene, indicating negligible charge transfer. In the island-covered system, the Fermi level shifts to lower energies, reflecting electron transfer from graphene to h-BN. These differences lead to distinct adsorption behavior. NO2 binds much more strongly to B11N11C72, forming a chemical bond, while O3 dissociates on this surface but remains intact on the extended bilayer. NH3 unusually acts as an electron acceptor in the island system. Overall, NO2 and O3 substantially increase graphene conductivity, whereas NH3 induces much weaker changes. These results highlight the potential of graphene/h-BN heterostructures for gas sensing.

</details>


### [73] [Radiation-induced segregation in dilute Fe-Cr: A rate-theory framework for the Cr enrichment-depletion transition at the grain boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14574)
*Russell Oplinger,Mukesh Bachhav,Karim Ahmed,Sourabh Bhagwan Kadambi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于物理的速率理论模型研究Fe-Cr合金中Cr在晶界的辐射诱导偏析，揭示了在对称点缺陷条件下温度决定偏析方向，而在非对称缺陷产生/吸收条件下偏析转变温度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射诱导偏析（RIS）对铁素体Fe-Cr合金的机械完整性和晶间腐蚀敏感性构成严重威胁，但目前对Cr偏析机制的理解尚不完整，需要建立物理模型来系统研究。

Method: 采用基于自洽平均场理论Onsager输运系数参数化的物理速率理论模型，研究稀Fe-(0.1 at.%) Cr合金中晶界处的RIS。在对称点缺陷产生和吸收条件下，系统分析温度、剂量率、晶粒尺寸和位错密度的影响，并考虑更实际的非对称缺陷条件。

Result: 在对称条件下，温度依赖的输运性质决定Cr偏析方向（低温富集→高温贫化），而剂量率、晶粒尺寸和位错密度仅影响偏析幅度和空间范围。在非对称缺陷产生/吸收条件下，富集-贫化转变温度向低温移动。

Conclusion: 仅基于输运系数的RIS预测仅在对称点缺陷通量条件下有效，实际预测必须考虑缺陷产生和吸收的非对称性。该工作为理解铁素体合金RIS提供了机制框架，对先进核系统合金设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Radiation-induced segregation (RIS) poses a significant challenge for ferritic Fe-Cr alloys under irradiation, as it can compromise mechanical integrity and increase susceptibility to intergranular corrosion. Yet, the mechanisms governing Cr segregation remain incompletely understood. In this study, We present a physics-based rate-theory model parameterized using self-consistent mean field theory-based Onsager transport coefficients to investigate RIS at the grain boundary (GB) in dilute Fe-(0.1 at.%) Cr. Under equal production rates of vacancies and self-interstitial atoms (SIA), and their equal absorption rates by bulk dislocations, the model simulates the experimentally observed transition from Cr enrichment at low temperatures to depletion at higher temperatures. Under these unbiased conditions, systematic investigation reveals that while temperature-dependent transport properties dictate the segregation direction, dose rate, grain size, and dislocation density only influence the magnitude and spatial extent of Cr segregation. However, under more realistic conditions of preferential vacancy production within damage cascade and/or preferential SIA absorption by bulk dislocations, the enrichment-to-depletion transition shifts to lower temperatures. Our findings demonstrate that RIS predictions based solely on transport coefficients are valid only under symmetric point defect flux conditions, and that biases in defect production and absorption must be considered for accurate predictions. This work provides a mechanistic framework for understanding RIS in ferritic alloys and informs alloy design for advanced nuclear systems.

</details>


### [74] [Sequential Quenching to Predict Semiconductor Defect Concentrations from Formation & Migration Energies: The Case of CdTe:As Doping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14618)
*Khandakar Aaditta Arnab,Intuon Chatratin,Anderson Janotti,Michael Scarpulla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出"顺序淬火(SQ)"作为第三种缺陷计算方法，考虑有限冷却速率下的扩散受限动力学，用于预测半导体中缺陷浓度随热历史的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缺陷计算通常假设瞬时淬火或完全平衡，这两种极限情况很少在现实中实现。实际半导体生长和冷却过程中的热历史会显著影响缺陷浓度，需要更符合物理实际的模型。

Method: 引入顺序淬火(SQ)方法，利用缺陷形成能和迁移能，模拟有限冷却速率下扩散受限的缺陷浓度变化。每个缺陷在特征温度下冻结，该温度由其扩散速率、到源/汇的距离和冷却速率决定。

Result: 以As掺杂CdTe为例，SQ计算显示：快速扩散缺陷(如Cd间隙)在较低温度下仍保持迁移性，在较大特征距离下冻结，导致快速冷却或块体样品中强烈补偿和n型行为。较慢冷却和较小特征距离抑制施主冻结，增强p型激活。

Conclusion: SQ提供了一个物理透明且计算高效的框架，将冷却条件、样品几何形状和缺陷动力学与CdTe及相关材料中的掺杂剂激活联系起来。并非所有室温SQ解都能从完全平衡或完全淬火计算中预测，没有SQ可能导致错误预测。

Abstract: Defect concentrations in semiconductors are strongly influenced by thermal history during growth and cooldown, yet most defect calculations assume either instantaneous quenching from high temperature or that full-equilibrium is maintained - two limiting cases rarely approached in reality. Here, we introduce sequential quenching (SQ) as a 3rd type of defect calculation utilizing defect formation and migration energies to model defect concentrations subject to diffusion-limited kinetics in samples cooled at finite rates. In SQ, the concentration of each defect is frozen at a characteristic temperature determined by its diffusion rate, distance to sources/sinks, and cooling rate. Because different charge-states interact through charge neutrality but freeze at different temperatures, the sequence of freeze-in events is non-commuting. Critically, not all room-temperature SQ solutions can be predicted from full equilibrium (EQ) or full-quenching (FQ) calculations - erroneous predictions are likely without SQ. We illustrate SQ using the example of As-doped CdTe, for which experimental data show differences in doping with cooling rate and between polycrystalline thin-films for photovoltaics and bulk crystals. SQ calculations reveal that fast-diffusing defects such as Cd-interstitials remain mobile to lower temperatures and freeze-in at larger characteristic distances, leading to strong compensation and n-type behavior in rapidly cooled or bulk samples. Slower cooling and reduced characteristic distances suppress donor freeze-in and enhance p-type activation. These results establish SQ as a physically transparent and computationally efficient framework for connecting cooling conditions, sample geometry, and defect kinetics to dopant activation in CdTe and related materials.

</details>


### [75] [Stabilization of the Orthorhombic Phase in Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 Nanoparticles by Oxygen Vacancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14642)
*Yuri O. Zagorodniy,Eugene A. Eliseev,Valentin V. Laguta,Petr Jiricek,Jana Houdkova,Lesya D. Demchenko,Oksana V. Leshchenko,Victor N. Pavlikov,Lesya P. Yurchenko,Anna O. Diachenko,Michail D. Volnyanskii,Myroslav V. Karpets,Mikhail P. Trubitsyn,Dean R. Evans,Anna N. Morozovska*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒中正交相(o-phase)的稳定机制，特别是氧空位浓度对相稳定的影响，这对于理解铁电/反铁电材料在纳米尺度下的相行为具有重要意义。

Method: 制备平均尺寸7nm的Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒，分别在空气和CO+CO2气氛中退火以调控氧空位浓度；使用电子顺磁共振(EPR)和X射线光电子能谱(XPS)测定氧空位浓度；通过X射线衍射(XRD)和核磁共振(NMR)表征正交相分数；基于Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire理论进行唯象计算分析化学应变对相稳定的作用。

Result: 正交相分数依赖于退火条件决定的氧空位浓度；理论计算证实氧空位诱导的化学应变能够稳定小尺寸铪锆氧化物纳米颗粒中具有极性和反极性长程有序的正交相。

Conclusion: 氧空位浓度是调控Hf0.5Zr0.5O2纳米颗粒中正交相稳定的关键因素，化学应变机制为理解纳米尺度下铁电/反铁电材料的相稳定提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this work we study the stabilization of the o-phase in small Hf0.5Zr0.5O2 nanoparticles (the average size 7 nm) annealed in air and in the CO+CO2 ambient. Concentration of the oxygen vacancies, which is determined by annealing conditions, was estimated from the electron paramagnetic resonance spectra and X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. The fraction of the orthorhombic phase that was controlled by the X-ray diffraction and nuclear magnetic resonance, depends on the concentration of oxygen vacancies, which are defined by annealing conditions. Phenomenological calculations based on Landau-Ginzburg-Devonshire theory confirm that the chemical strains induced by oxygen vacancies can stabilize the orthorhombic phase with polar and antipolar long-range ordering in small hafnia-zirconia nanoparticles.

</details>


### [76] [A phase field model with arbitrary misorientation dependence of grain boundary energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14660)
*Philip Staublin,Yuri Mishin,Peter W. Voorhees*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种改进的取向场模型，通过使自由能泛函系数成为晶粒间非局部取向差的函数，从而能够嵌入任意取向差依赖的晶界自由能，克服了现有模型无法再现晶界自由能随取向差增大而降低的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有取向场模型难以真实地表示晶界自由能对取向差的依赖性，特别是无法再现晶界自由能随取向差增大而降低的现象，这在多晶材料应用中存在显著局限性。

Method: 对Kobayashi-Warren-Carter晶粒生长模型进行改进，使自由能泛函系数成为晶粒间非局部取向差的函数。通过沿局部晶界法向矢量在两个方向上固定距离处插值取向场来计算非局部取向差。

Result: 改进后的模型能够嵌入任意取向差依赖的晶界自由能，包括尖锐的尖点依赖关系。通过引入尖锐尖点展示了模型的能力，并提出了向三维扩展的方案。

Conclusion: 提出的改进取向场模型克服了现有模型在表示晶界自由能取向差依赖性方面的根本限制，为多晶材料中更真实的晶粒生长模拟提供了有效工具，并可扩展到三维应用。

Abstract: Grain growth in polycrystals is often simulated using orientation-field models, which employ a field to represent the local orientation of the crystal lattice. These models can be challenging to represent a realistic misorientation dependence of grain boundary free energy. We prove that existing orientation-field models, in general, cannot reproduce a decrease in the grain boundary free energy with a increasing misorientation angle, demonstrating a significant limitation of previous models in applications to polycrystalline materials. To overcome this limitation, we propose a modification to the Kobayashi-Warren-Carter model for grain growth wherein the coefficients of the free-energy functional become functions of the misorientation between the grains, which is a non-local quantity. Due to this modification, an arbitrary dependence of the grain boundary free energy on the misorientation can be embedded in the model. We propose calculating the non-local misorientation by interpolating the orientation field at a fixed distance in both directions along the local grain boundary normal vector. The capabilities of the model are demonstrated by introduction of a sharp cusp to the misorientation dependent grain boundary free energy. Finally, we propose an extension of the model to three dimensions.

</details>


### [77] [Decoupling structural and bonding effects on ferroelectric switching in ScAlN via molecular dynamics under an applied electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14747)
*Ryotaro Sahashi,Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ScxAl1-xN的剩余极化强度由结构效应（内部结构参数u）线性决定，而矫顽场则由结构效应和键合效应（Sc含量增加导致键弱化）共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScxAl1-xN作为有前景的纤锌矿型铁电材料，其Sc含量增加会同时降低矫顽场和剩余极化强度。实验上结构效应（内部结构参数u增加）和键合效应（键强度减弱）强烈耦合，难以区分各自对铁电翻转的贡献。

Method: 使用机器学习力场分子动力学模拟在电场作用下系统解耦两种效应：1）通过面内应变人工调控u（固定成分）；2）评估相同u但不同Sc成分的构型；3）静态推弹性能带计算分析静态翻转势垒。

Result: 剩余极化强度仅由结构效应决定，与u呈普适线性关系，与成分无关；矫顽场偏离结构趋势，额外受键合效应影响；相同u下，Pr保持不变而Ec随Sc增加系统降低；静态翻转势垒仅取决于u，无法显式捕获键合效应对Ec的影响。

Conclusion: ScxAl1-xN中剩余极化强度严格由结构效应控制，而矫顽场由结构效应和键合效应叠加决定。研究强调了动态分子动力学模拟对于完全理解成分可调材料中铁电翻转的必要性。

Abstract: ScxAl1-xN has emerged as a promising wurtzite-type ferroelectric material, where increasing the Sc composition reduces both the coercive field (Ec) and remanent polarization (Pr). This composition-dependent behavior is physically attributed to two simultaneous changes: the increase in the internal structural parameter u (structural effect) and the weakening of bond strength (bonding effect). Because these factors are strongly coupled in experiments, their individual contributions to ferroelectric switching remain unclear. In this study, we systematically decoupled these effects using machine-learning force field-based molecular dynamics (MD) simulations under an applied electric field. By artificially tuning u via in-plane strain at a fixed composition, we demonstrated that Pr is determined exclusively by the structural effect, exhibiting a universal linear dependence regardless of the composition. In contrast, Ec deviated from this structural trend, implying an additional compositional contribution. To isolate this, we evaluated configurations with identical u but varying Sc compositions; Pr remained constant, whereas Ec systematically decreased due to bond weakening. Furthermore, static nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations revealed that the static switching barrier depends solely on u, failing to explicitly capture the bonding effect on Ec. These results establish that while Pr is governed strictly by the structural effect, Ec is determined by a superposition of structural and bonding effects. Our findings highlight the necessity of dynamic MD simulations for fully understanding ferroelectric switching in compositionally tunable materials.

</details>


### [78] [Synchrotron-radiation X-ray topography and reticulography of bulk $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ crystals grown from a crucible-free melt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14751)
*Yongzhao Yao,Koki Mizuno,Kazuki Ohnishi,Yukari Ishikawa,Masanori Kitahara,Taketoshi Tomida,Rikito Murakami,Vladimir Kochurikhin,Liudmila Gushchina,Kei Kamada,Koichi Kakimoto,Akira Yoshikawa*

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线形貌术和X射线网状术分析晶体结构，通过摇摆曲线半高宽评估晶体质量，分析晶格取向变化和位错类型与密度。

Result: 种子下方区域晶体质量高（摇摆曲线半高宽~26角秒）；直径扩大时中心与侧向扩展区域间出现扭曲型晶格取向差；<010>取向的螺位错占主导（密度~10⁵ cm⁻²），翼区密度更高（~10⁶ cm⁻²）。

Conclusion: 阐明了OCCC法生长β-Ga₂O₃中的缺陷形成机制，为优化生长条件提供了重要见解，特别是控制直径扩大过程中的晶格取向变化和位错密度分布。

Abstract: The structural properties of a $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ single crystal grown by the oxide crystal growth from cold crucible (OCCC) method were investigated using synchrotron radiation X-ray topography and X-ray reticulography. The region grown beneath the seed exhibits high crystalline quality with a rocking curve full width at half maximum of about 26 arcsec. During diameter enlargement, a twist-type lattice misorientation develops between the central and laterally expanded regions, originating near the shoulder and propagating along boundaries parallel to the $\langle010\rangle$ growth direction. Dislocation analysis reveals that $\langle010\rangle$-oriented screw dislocations dominate the defect structure with densities of ~$10^{5}$cm$^{-2}$, while higher densities (~$10^{6}$cm$^{-2}$) appear in the wing region. These results clarify defect formation in OCCC-grown $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ and provide insights for optimizing growth conditions.

</details>


### [79] [Hybrid Tribo/piezoelectic Electrospun Nanofibers for Energy Harvesting Enhancement in Flexible Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14857)
*Hao Zhang,Yurong He,Yaofeng Jin,Hui Wang,Wanqi Ye,Lidong Chen,Kaiyang Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过电纺制备PVDF基纳米纤维，并引入碳纳米管或石墨烯纳米片作为纳米填料，开发了一种兼具摩擦电和压电效应的混合纳米发电机，实现了高达1.133 W/m²的功率密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦电纳米发电机（TENGs）和压电纳米发电机（PENGs）是收集机械能并为柔性电子设备供电的有前景平台，但混合纳米发电机的材料选择、结构设计和能量输出机制仍存在挑战。

Method: 采用电纺技术制备PVDF基纳米纤维，利用其产生高β相含量的能力提升压电性能；分别将不同浓度的碳纳米管（CNT）或石墨烯纳米片（GNS）作为纳米填料掺入PVDF溶液，以增强压电响应并改变纤维直径和表面粗糙度，从而同时提升摩擦电效应。

Result: 纳米填料的引入不仅改变了纤维直径和表面粗糙度，还显著提升了β相含量（最高达85.3%）；发电机的输出性能主要由压电效应主导，与β相含量呈强正相关；最优能量输出达到1.133 W/m²，功率密度达到W/m²量级，相比传统TENGs或PENGs有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过电纺PVDF基纳米纤维并引入纳米填料，成功开发了高性能摩擦-压电混合纳米发电机，其功率密度达到W/m²级别，为柔性电子设备提供了更广阔的应用机会。

Abstract: Triboelectric nanogenerators or TENGs and piezoelectric nanogenerators or PENGs have emerged as promising platforms for harvesting mechanical energy and converting it into electrical energy for powering flexible electronic devices. However, the material selection and structure design of such hybrid nanogenerator, and mechanisms of energy output still remain challenges. In this work, electrospinning is employed for the fabrication of nanofibers, particularly polyvinylidene fluoride or PVDF based nanofibers, due to its capability to generate high beta phase contents that effectively increase the piezoelectric performance of the PVDF friction layer, thereby enhancing the overall electrical performance for flexible electronics by merging tribo-piezoelectric power. Furthermore, various concentrations carbon nanotubes or CNT or graphene nanosheets or GNS are individually incorporated into the PVDF solution as nanofillers or NF to enhance the piezoelectric responses of the PVDF based nanofibers. The introduction of nanofillers is found to not only alter the fiber diameter but also modify the surface roughness of the electrospun nanofibers, and thus, enhancing the triboelectric effect. In addition, the output performance of the fabricated nanogenerator is predominantly governed by the piezoelectric effect rather than triboelectric effect, as the electrical output shows a strong positive correlation with the beta phase content of PVDF based nanofibers: the highest beta phase content reached to 85.3 percent and consistently resulted in the optimal energy output of 1.133 watt per meter square. Notably, the power density achieved by the prototype device reaches to the level of watt/m2, representing a substantial improvement compared with that of the conventional TENGs or PENGs reported to date, providing expanded opportunities for flexible electronic devices

</details>


### [80] [Scalar Spin Chiral Order via Bond Selectivity in Strained Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14971)
*Xin Liu,Li Ma,Mingyue Zhao,Shun Niu,Yu Liu,Yang Li,Jiayao Zhu,Yiwen Zhang,Fengxian Ma,Dewei Zhao,Guoke Li,Congmian Zhen,Denglu Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 应变可诱导高温共线磁体Mn4N产生长程标量自旋手性序，实现从共线到非共面磁结构的连续转变，为高温拓扑磁体设计提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量自旋手性(SSC)在非共面磁体中驱动拓扑输运，但现有SSC有序温度通常低于100K。当前实现近室温SSC的方法主要依赖外场或化学掺杂，在高温共线磁体中生成和控制SSC序仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 以Neel温度~740K的共线亚铁磁体Mn4N为平台，通过第一性原理计算研究各向同性应变作为清洁连续调控参数诱导长程SSC序的机制。采用定量轨道分辨键合分析揭示应变对Mn 3d轨道与N 2p轨道键合的调控作用。

Result: 应变从0增加到特定值时，磁基态从共线连续演变为非共面构型，激活SSC序并将其幅度从0提升至~2.32。应变选择性抑制Mn 3d与N 2p轨道间的键合，降低共价自旋配对激活平面内Mn3c磁矩，同时抑制N介导的铁磁超交换相互作用，使最近邻Mn3c位点间的交换相互作用平衡向反铁磁方向移动。

Conclusion: 研究建立了应变介导的强有力途径，可在高温共线磁体中构建SSC序，为高温拓扑磁体设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Scalar spin chirality (SSC) drives a series of topological transports in noncoplanar magnets. However, the ordering temperature of magnet hosting intrinsic SSC order is typically below 100 K. Current approaches to achieve near room temperature SSC order largely rely on external fields or chemical doping in noncollinear magnets. A significant challenge persists in generating and controlling SSC order in high temperature collinear magnets. Here, using the collinear ferrimagnet Mn4N with Neel temperature ~740 K as a platform, we demonstrate that isotropic strain acts as a clean and continuous tuning parameter to induce long range SSC order by first principles calculations. As strain increases from to, the magnetic ground state evolves continuously from a collinear to a noncoplanar configuration, activating the SSC order and enhancing its magnitude from 0 to ~2.32. Our quantitative orbital-resolved bonding analysis reveals that strain selectively suppresses the bond between Mn 3d orbitals and N 2p orbitals, driving dual prerequisites for the SSC order. Specifically, the decreased covalent spin-pairing activates Mn3c moments within the plane, simultaneously the suppressed N-mediated ferromagnetic superexchange interaction shifts the balance of the nearest-neighbor Mn3c sites toward antiferromagnetic exchange interaction. Our findings establish a powerful strain mediated route to construct the SSC order in high temperature collinear magnets.

</details>


### [81] [Electric-Field-induced Two-Dimensional Fully Compensated Ferrimagnetism and Emergent Transport Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14996)
*Jin-Yang Li,Yong-Kun Wang,Ying Zhang,Si Li,Wen-Li Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 单层CoS和CoSe在外加电场下可转变为完全补偿铁磁态，展现出零净磁化下的自旋分裂能带、全自旋极化电流、反常霍尔效应及磁光效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全补偿铁磁系统虽然具有零净磁化和自旋分裂能带等与altermagnetism相似的特征，但研究较少。本文旨在探索单层CoS和CoSe在外电场作用下实现fFIM态的可能性及其在自旋电子学中的应用潜力。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算和理论分析，研究单层CoS和CoSe在外加垂直电场下的磁性和电子结构变化。分析电场对PT对称性的破坏效应及其对自旋分裂的影响。

Result: 单层CoS和CoSe的基态为具有面外Néel矢量的共线反铁磁态，电子能带因PT对称性而自旋简并。外加垂直电场破坏PT对称性，诱导出具有显著自旋分裂的fFIM态。该态表现出全自旋极化电流、反常霍尔效应、磁光Kerr和Faraday效应。

Conclusion: 单层CoS和CoSe是实现电场调控完全补偿铁磁态的理想平台，在自旋电子学应用中具有重要潜力，为探索零净磁化下的自旋相关现象提供了新途径。

Abstract: The recent discovery of altermagnetism has demonstrated that spin-split electronic band structures can emerge in magnetic systems with zero net magnetization. In contrast, fully compensated ferrimagnetic (fFIM) systems remain far less explored, despite exhibiting similar characteristics such as vanishing magnetization and spin-split bands. Here, based on first-principles calculations combined with theoretical analysis, we demonstrate that monolayer CoS and CoSe can be driven into fFIM states by an external electric field. These materials possess collinear antiferromagnetic ground states with out-of-plane Néel vectors, and their electronic bands are spin degenerate due to $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry. When an out-of-plane electric field is applied, $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry is broken, inducing fFIM states with pronounced spin splitting. Moreover, we show that the resulting fFIM states host fully spin-polarized currents, anomalous Hall effects, and magneto-optical Kerr and Faraday effects. Our results establish monolayer CoS and CoSe as promising platforms for electric-field-controlled fFIM states and spintronic applications.

</details>


### [82] [Domain Walls Stabilized by Intrinsic Phonon Modes and Engineered Defects Enable Robust Ferroelectricity in HfO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15041)
*Chenxi Yu,Jiajia Zhang,Xujin Song,Dijiang Sun,Shangze Li,Fei Liu,Xiaoyan Liu,Wei Xi,Jinfeng Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于声子模式展开的统一框架揭示了HfO₂铁电体中界面声子模式对畴壁稳定性的关键作用，以及缺陷对铁电畴的钉扎和稳定机制，为掺杂和缺陷工程增强HfO₂铁电性提供了微观物理起源的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电HfO₂在AI时代具有重要应用价值，但畴壁在相结构稳定和极化翻转中的关键作用尚未得到充分理解，需要从微观物理机制层面深入研究。

Method: 开发了基于声子模式展开的统一理论框架，结合第一性原理计算，系统研究声子模式和缺陷对畴壁结构的影响。

Result: 发现界面声子模式对畴壁稳定性起关键作用；缺陷能够钉扎和稳定铁电畴，从而稳定亚稳正交相并促进极化翻转；理论预测的畴结构和缺陷分布在La掺杂HfO₂铁电薄膜的EELS和STEM实验中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为掺杂和缺陷工程增强HfO₂铁电性提供了微观物理机制的理解，理论预测与实验观测的一致性验证了研究方法的有效性，为HfO₂铁电材料的优化设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ has attracted extensive research interest for its applications in AI era. The domain walls play a crucial role in phase structure stabilization and polarization switching of ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$, however, a thorough understanding is still lacking. Here, we developed a unified framework based on phonon mode expansion to systematically study the effects of phonon modes and defects on domain wall structures. Using this approach combined with first-principle calculations, we revealed that the interface phonon modes play a key role in stability of domain walls; defects pin and stabilize ferroelectric domains, which in turn stabilizes the metastable orthorhombic phase and facilitates polarization switching. This provides an insight from the microscopic physics origin into the enhanced ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ by doping and defect engineering. Furthermore, the theoretically predicted domain structures and defect distributions were observed in La-doped $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ ferroelectric films by EELS and STEM experiments, which confirms the validity of our findings.

</details>


### [83] [Persistent incommensurate amorphous/crystalline meta-interfaces enable engineering-grade superlubricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15089)
*Wan Wang,Zijun Ding,Panpan Li,Wanying Ying,Hongxuan Li,Xiaohong Liu,Huidi Zhou,Jianmin Chen,Wengen Ouyang,Li Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于非晶/晶体异质界面的材料无关策略，实现工程条件下鲁棒超润滑，摩擦系数~0.008，在毫米尺度、12.7 GPa接触压力、40%湿度空气中保持10万次循环


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩擦消耗全球大量能源，追求超润滑状态。传统方法依赖晶格失配抑制周期性势垒，但实际界面存在缺陷、边缘和晶界，会恢复高摩擦状态

Method: 采用非晶/晶体异质界面策略，以类金刚石碳(DLC)和MoS2为模型系统。通过激光图案化制备DLC/MoS2元接触阵列，并用Ti3C2Tx MXene增强，形成分层界面结构

Result: DLC/MoS2界面在所有取向上保持非公度状态，摩擦时能量势垒消失。而扭曲MoS2双层易重排为公度高摩擦状态。制备的分层界面在毫米尺度、12.7 GPa接触压力、40%湿度空气中，摩擦系数~0.008，保持10万次循环

Conclusion: 建立了通用设计范式，将结构超润滑从纳米尺度模型系统扩展到可持续工程实用技术，基于非晶/晶体界面固有非公度性、DLC支撑刚性、MXene机械增强和几何图案化归一化载荷分布

Abstract: Friction dissipates a substantial portion of global energy, motivating the pursuit of superlubricity, a state of near-zero friction, in real-world systems. Conventional approaches rely on crystalline lattice mismatch to suppress periodic energy barriers, but real interfaces invariably contain defects, edges and grain boundaries that restore high-friction states. Here we introduce a materials-agnostic strategy based on amorphous/crystalline heterointerfaces to achieve robust superlubricity under engineering-relevant conditions. Using diamond-like carbon (DLC) and crystalline MoS2 as a model system, we show through experiments and atomistic simulations that their interface remains incommensurate at all orientations and exhibits vanishing energy barriers during friction. In contrast, twisted MoS2 bilayers readily reorient into commensurate, high-friction states. We scale this effect by fabricating laser-patterned arrays of DLC/MoS2 meta-contacts reinforced with Ti3C2Tx MXene, forming hierarchical interfaces that sustain a friction coefficient of ~0.008 over 100000 cycles under combined extreme conditions: millimetre-scale contact size, 12.7 GPa contact pressure and RH 40% air. This unprecedented performance arises from four synergistic factors: intrinsic incommensurability at amorphous/crystalline interface, the rigidity of DLC support, MXene-based mechanical reinforcement and normalized load distribution by geometric patterning. These findings establish a general design paradigm that extends structural superlubricity from nanoscale model systems to practical technologies for sustainable engineering.

</details>


### [84] [Probing a two-dimensional soft ferromagnet Cr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$ by a tuning fork resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15123)
*Hengrui Gui,Zekai Shi,Jiawen Zhang,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan,Lin Jiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用石英音叉谐振器测量Cr₂Ge₂Te₆的磁各向异性，揭示其准二维易轴铁磁特性，为音叉磁转矩测量提供理想参考系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁各向异性是理解磁性材料自由能景观的关键，但传统磁测量方法难以定量表征。需要开发新的探测技术来直接获取磁转矩磁化率。

Method: 采用石英音叉谐振器技术测量Cr₂Ge₂Te₆的温度、磁场和角度依赖性磁转矩磁化率响应。通过准二维易轴铁磁模型对数据进行系统分析。

Result: 测量结果显示磁转矩磁化率随温度、磁场和角度变化，从传统的cos(2θ)形式演变为明显的凹陷结构。证实Cr₂Ge₂Te₆是理想的音叉磁转矩测量参考系统。

Conclusion: 音叉谐振器为各向异性低维磁体提供了灵敏的热力学探针，能够区分自旋起源各向异性和轨道磁性，为相关材料研究提供了有用框架。

Abstract: Magnetic anisotropy encodes key information about the free-energy landscape of magnetic materials, but its quantitative characterization often requires probes beyond conventional magnetometry. A quartz tuning-fork resonator provides direct access to the magnetotropic susceptibility. Here we use this technique to investigate the magnetic anisotropy of the layered ferromagnet Cr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$. The temperature-, field-, and angle-dependent responses are consistently described by a quasi-two-dimensional (2D) easy-axis ferromagnetic model. In particular, the evolution of the magnetotropic susceptibility reveals how the angular profile changes from a conventional cos(2$θ$) form to a pronounced dip structure as the magnetization approaches directional saturation. These results establishCr$_2$Ge$_2$Te$_6$ as an ideal reference system for tuning-fork-based magnetotropic measurements. More broadly, they provide a useful framework for distinguishing spin-origin anisotropy from orbital magnetism, as in the case of CsV3Sb5. Our work demonstrates that tuning-fork resonators offer a sensitive thermodynamic probe of the rotational stiffness of magnetization in anisotropic low-dimensional magnets.

</details>


### [85] [Monolithic integration of diverse crystalline thin films on diamond for near-junction thermal management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15142)
*Tiancheng Zhao,Tianqi Bai,Yang He,Wenhui Xu,Xinxin Yu,Ruochen Shi,Zhenyu Qu,Jiaxin Liu,Rui Shen,Haodong Jiang,Yeliang Wang,Jiaxin Ding,Dongchen Sui,Shibin Zhang,Lei Zhu,Ailun Yi,Kai Huang,Min Zhou,Huarui Sun,Zhonghui Li,Peng Gao,Tiangui You,Xin Ou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过多步转移印刷技术将多种单晶薄膜集成到金刚石衬底上，实现了创纪录的低热阻β-Ga2O3 MOSFET，解决了6G射频前端的热管理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G射频前端追求极致小型化和高功率，热耗散成为核心挑战。需要解决近结热管理问题，特别是高功率器件在金刚石衬底上的热传导瓶颈。

Method: 采用多步转移印刷技术将β-Ga2O3、Si、GaN和LiTaO3等不同功能的单晶薄膜单片集成到金刚石衬底上。通过超高真空退火处理β-Ga2O3/金刚石界面，实现原子级尖锐界面和共价键合。结合振动电子能量损失谱和分子动力学模拟分析界面声子模式。

Result: 实现了149 MW m⁻² K⁻¹的优异界面热导率，β-Ga2O3 MOSFET的热阻达到创纪录的1.58 K mm W⁻¹。研究发现β-Ga2O3/金刚石异质界面的独特界面声子模式主导了超高界面热导。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个可扩展的金刚石基单片集成平台，通过界面工程解决了高功率射频前端的近结热管理挑战，为6G器件热设计提供了关键解决方案。

Abstract: The pursuit of extreme miniaturization and high power in 6G RF front-ends has cast thermal dissipation as the central challenge. Here, we have demonstrated the monolithic integration of functionally distinct single-crystal thin films, including \b{eta}-Ga2O3, Si, GaN, and LiTaO3, onto a single diamond substrate using a multi-step transfer printing technique. Focusing on the critical \b{eta}-Ga2O3/diamond interface, we achieve an exceptional interfacial thermal conductance (ITC) of 149 MW m-2 K-1 through ultra-high vacuum (UHV) annealing, creating an atomically sharp interface featuring covalent bonding. Vibrational electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS) analysis combining with molecular dynamics (MD) simulations reveal that distinctive interfacial phonon modes at the \b{eta}-Ga2O3/diamond heterointerface dominate ultrahigh ITC. We experimentally demonstrate that by improving the ITC, the thermal resistance (Rth) of a diamond-based \b{eta}-Ga2O3 MOSFET is driven to a record-low value of 1.58 K mm W-1, underscoring the critical role of interface engineering in near-junction thermal management for diamond-integrated devices. This work demonstrates a scalable, diamond-based monolithic integration platform designed to solve the near-junction thermal challenges in high-power RF front-ends.

</details>


### [86] [Decoding the Complexity of Ferroelectric Orthorhombic HfO2: A Unified Mode Expansion Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15162)
*Chenxi Yu,Jiajia Zhang,Xujin Song,Fei Liu,Jinfeng Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于声子模展开的统一框架，用于研究HfO₂铁电性，将正交晶系相结构、畴壁和翻转路径统一到模分析基础上，简化了低对称性OIII相的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HfO₂薄膜的铁电性通常归因于极性正交OIII相的形成，但该相的低对称性复杂性阻碍了对HfO₂铁电性质的研究，需要开发简化研究框架。

Method: 开发基于声子模展开的统一框架，将正交相（包括OIII相）表示为高对称性立方相加上立方声子模激发，通过模的能量泛函分析正交相稳定性，枚举不等价畴壁并计算其稳定判据，总结所有可能的翻转机制。

Result: 阐明了正交相稳定性的起源，枚举了不等价畴壁并建立了稳定判据，总结了所有可能的翻转机制，为研究畴壁结构和相变路径提供了简化方法。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够简化畴壁结构和相变路径的研究，为从声子模分析角度理解HfO₂铁电性提供了新视角。

Abstract: The ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ thin films is widely attributed to the formation of a polar orthorhombic phase named OIII phase. However, the complexity of OIII phase originated from its low symmetry becomes an obstacle for studying ferroelectric properties of $\mathrm{HfO}_2$. Here, we developed a unified framework based on phonon mode expansion for studying ferroelectric $\mathrm{HfO}_2$. In this framework, phase structures, domain walls and switching paths of orthogonal crystal system can be studied from the same basis of mode analysis. The OIII phase and other orthogonal phases can be represented by the high-symmetry cubic phase with the excitation of cubic phonon modes, into which the complexity of orthogonal phases is faithfully coded. To present the capability of this mode expansion approach, we clarified the origin of orthorhombic stability from the energy functional of modes; enumerated inequivalent domain walls and calculated their stable criteria; and summarized all possible switching mechanisms. This unified framework can be used to simplify the study of domain wall structures and transition paths. Furthermore, it can provide a new perspective for ferroelectricity in $\mathrm{HfO}_2$ from phonon mode analysis.

</details>


### [87] [A biomimetic feedback loop for sustaining self-lubrication and wear resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15181)
*Fuyan Kang,Shilin Deng,Panpan Li,Rui Zhao,Xiaohong Liu,Hongxuan Li,Huidi Zhou,Jianmin Chen,Wengen Ouyang,Li Ji*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Cu(Au)/C纳米复合薄膜作为仿生自调节润滑剂，利用摩擦热触发软金属纳米颗粒熔化和迁移，在摩擦界面原位催化形成有序碳纳米结构，实现自限性反馈循环，获得超低摩擦系数(~0.04)和超长磨损寿命(>40 km)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够自我感知和自我调节的智能材料是可持续技术的前沿领域。传统润滑材料缺乏自适应能力，无法根据摩擦条件动态调整性能。本研究旨在创建一种仿生自调节润滑系统，利用摩擦产生的热量作为内在激活信号，实现材料的自主性能优化。

Method: 制备Cu(Au)/C纳米复合薄膜作为自调节润滑剂。当摩擦系数升高产生热量时，软金属纳米颗粒（Cu或Au）熔化并沿纳米孔迁移至摩擦界面。迁移的金属催化原位形成有序碳纳米结构，降低摩擦系数。通过实时监测摩擦系数、电阻和金属释放来验证自调节机制。

Result: 该系统实现了自限性反馈循环：高摩擦系数产生热量→熔化金属纳米颗粒→金属迁移降低摩擦系数→热量减少停止进一步迁移→摩擦再次升高时循环继续。获得了稳定的超低摩擦系数(~0.04)和卓越的磨损寿命(>40 km)，即使在真空条件下也能保持性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用摩擦热作为内在激活信号，该系统建立了一个通用的智能自调节材料范式。这种仿生自调节机制不仅限于摩擦学应用，还可扩展到其他需要自适应性能的材料系统，为可持续智能材料的发展开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Intelligent materials that self-sense and self-regulate are an emerging frontier in sustainable technology. Here we introduce Cu(Au)/C nanocomposite films that act as bioinspired self-adjusting lubricants. In these films, frictional heating triggers melting and migration of soft metal nanoparticles (NPs) such as Cu or Au along nano-pores to the friction interface, where the metal catalyzes the in-situ formation of ordered carbon nano-structures. Real-time monitoring of friction coefficient, electrical resistance(R), and metal release confirms an autonomous cycle: high friction coefficient generates heat, melting the metal NPs; the migrating metal then lowers friction coefficent by creating low-friction nanostructures, which reduces heat and arrests further migration until friction rises again. This self-limiting feedback enables stable ultra-low friction (~0.04) and an exceptional wear life (>40 km) even in high vacuum. By utilizing friction-derived heat as an intrinsic activation signal, our system establishes a general paradigm for intelligent, self-regulating materials with applications extending beyond tribology.

</details>


### [88] [Will it form a glass? Tackling glass formation using binary classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15312)
*Diogo P. L. Carvalho,Ana C. B. Loponi,Daniel R. Cassar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用随机森林分类器预测无机非金属液体的玻璃形成能力，在超过5万个样本的数据集上取得了ROC-AUC约0.89和PR-AUC约0.95的性能，并通过SHAP值分析发现元素带隙能量与玻璃形成正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃形成是玻璃科学中最重要且尚未解决的基础问题之一。预测液体能否在典型实验室条件下形成玻璃看似简单实则困难，本研究旨在通过机器学习方法解决这一挑战。

Method: 采用二元分类方法，使用超过5万个样本的数据集训练随机森林分类器。实施了严格的模型选择流程，包括交叉验证的超参数调优，并评估了四种不同的数据处理路径。使用SHAP值进行模型解释。

Result: 模型在未见数据上取得了ROC-AUC约0.89和PR-AUC约0.95的优秀性能。通过SHAP分析发现元素带隙能量与玻璃形成呈正相关。添加玻璃稳定性参数和Jezica参数并未提升性能，但显著降低了模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了预测无机非金属液体玻璃形成能力的机器学习模型，不仅验证了已有知识，还发现了新的相关性（如元素带隙能量的重要性）。模型在成分筛选和逆向设计问题中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Glass formation is one of the most important and fundamental open problems in glass science. Predicting whether a liquid can be easily frozen into a glass appears simple but is far from it. In this communication, we address glass formation in inorganic nonmetallic liquids using binary classification to predict the probability that a given liquid will form a glass under typical laboratory conditions. Using a dataset of more than 50,000 examples, we trained random forest classifiers that achieved ROC-AUC values around 0.89 and PR-AUC close to 0.95 on the holdout dataset (i.e., unseen data). A rigorous model selection routine was employed, including hyperparameter tuning with cross-validation, and four different data treatment routes were evaluated. Using SHAP values, we extracted valuable insights from the trained models that both agree with established knowledge and extend it. For example, we identified that the bandgap energy of the constituent chemical elements is positively correlated with glass formation. When glass stability parameters and Jezica were added to the dataset, no performance improvement was observed, but model complexity decreased significantly. This result is particularly relevant for composition screening, especially in inverse design problems.

</details>


### [89] [Coupled Ferroelectricity and Phonon Chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15325)
*Xiang-Bin Han,Cong Yang,Rui Sun,Xiaotong Zhang,Thuc Mai,Zhengze Xu,Aryan Jouneghaninaseri,Xiaoning Jiang,Rahul Rao,Yi Xia,Dali Sun,Jun Liu,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 实验证明分子铁电体硫酸三甘氨酸中铁电性与手性声子耦合，通过电场切换晶体手性实现声子手性的可逆控制，为基于铁电材料的手性声子自旋控制技术开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手性材料（如石英）的手性声子由其固有手性决定且无法切换，限制了手性相关量子态的控制。铁电材料具有可逆切换的自发极化，若手性与铁电极化耦合，则可实现电控晶体手性和声子角动量，兼容固态自旋电子学架构。

Method: 使用分子铁电体硫酸三甘氨酸，通过电场切换晶体手性；采用原位时间分辨磁光克尔效应测量揭示声子手性控制；结合密度泛函理论计算和圆偏振拉曼光谱验证相反的圆形声子运动。

Result: 克尔旋转随电场切换而反转；声子手性在顺电相消失，在外消旋铁电态可调；实验证实了铁电性与声子手性的耦合，实现了声子手性的可逆、器件兼容控制。

Conclusion: 建立了铁电性、结构手性、手性声子和自旋之间的电可寻址耦合通路，为基于铁电材料的手性声子自旋和声子控制技术开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The ability to control chirality and chiral phonons offers a route to manipulate the direction of spin and angular-momentum transport. In materials with rigid structural chirality, such as quartz, phonon chirality is fixed by the handedness and cannot be switched. By contrast, ferroelectric materials host a spontaneous polarization that can be reversibly switched by an external electric field. When chirality is coupled to this ferroelectric polarization, it enables electrical switching of crystal chirality and the associated phonon angular momentum, which is compatible with solid-state spintronic architectures, enabling control over chirality-dependent quantum states.1 Here, we report the experimental demonstration of the coupling between ferroelectricity and phonon chirality in the molecular ferroelectric triglycine sulfate. By electrically switching the crystal chirality, we achieve reversible and device-compatible control of phonon chirality, as revealed by in situ time-resolved magneto-optical Kerr effect measurements. The Kerr rotation reverses with electric-field switching, while phonon chirality vanishes in the paraelectric phase and is tunable in the racemic ferroelectric state. Furthermore, density functional theory calculations and circularly polarized Raman spectroscopy further corroborate the opposite circular phonon motions. These results establish an electrically addressable coupling pathway linking ferroelectricity, structural chirality, chiral phonons, and spin, opening a route toward chiral-phonon-enabled spin and phonon control technologies based on ferroelectric materials.

</details>


### [90] [Polar Charge-Ordered States in BiFeO$_3$/CaFeO$_3$ Superlattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15362)
*Rajan Gowsalya,Monirul Shaikh,Sathiyamoorthy Buvaneswaran,Saurabh Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: BiFeO₃/CaFeO₃超晶格通过界面电荷转移和晶格畸变工程，实现了结合C型反铁磁性和铁电半导体行为的非中心对称Pc相，带隙约0.6 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物超晶格为通过界面电荷转移和晶格畸变调控电子相提供了有前景的途径。研究BiFeO₃/CaFeO₃超晶格的结构和电子性质，旨在探索铁酸盐超晶格作为多功能材料平台的潜力。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算和对称模式分析相结合的方法，研究BiFeO₃/CaFeO₃超晶格的结构和电子性质。分析八面体旋转和FeO₆呼吸畸变等晶格不稳定性及其协同耦合效应。

Result: 极性BiFeO₃和电荷转移型CaFeO₃的结合产生了强烈的晶格不稳定性，其协同耦合稳定了非中心对称的Pc基态，该相具有Fe离子的极性电荷有序。该相结合了C型反铁磁性和铁电半导体行为，间接带隙约为0.6 eV。

Conclusion: 铁酸盐超晶格是一个多功能材料设计平台，通过界面和应变工程可以可控地调控极化、电荷有序和电子输运性质，为多功能材料设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Oxide superlattices provide a promising route for engineering electronic phases through interfacial charge transfer and lattice distortions. Here, we investigate the structural and electronic properties of the BiFeO$_3$/CaFeO$_3$ superlattice using a combination of first-principles computations and symmetry-mode analysis. The incorporation of polar bismuth ferrite together with charge-transfer calcium ferrite generates strong lattice instabilities involving octahedral rotations and FeO$_6$ breathing distortions. Their cooperative coupling stabilizes a non-centrosymmetric $Pc$ ground state characterized by polar charge ordering of Fe ions. The resulting phase combines C-type antiferromagnetism with ferroelectric semiconductor behavior, featuring an indirect band gap of about 0.6 eV. Our findings establish ferrite superlattices as a versatile platform for designing multifunctional materials where polarization, charge ordering, and electronic transport can be controllably manipulated through interface and strain engineering.

</details>


### [91] [Epitaxial growth of topological insulator $β$-Ag2Te thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15406)
*Ayuki Takegawa,Kouya Imoto,Minoru Kawamura,Moeta Tsukamoto,Ryutaro Yoshimi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过分子束外延在InP衬底上生长β-Ag2Te拓扑绝缘体薄膜，实现二维金属性表面态与绝缘体体的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-Ag2Te作为新近发现的拓扑绝缘体，其表面存在狄拉克态，但高质量外延薄膜的生长方法尚未建立，限制了对其表面态物理性质的研究和异质结器件的开发。

Method: 采用分子束外延技术，先在室温下在InP衬底上沉积Ag，然后在升高温度下供应Te，通过X射线衍射和透射电子显微镜表征晶体结构和外延关系，并进行电输运测量。

Result: 成功生长出(002)取向的β-Ag2Te外延薄膜，电输运测量显示薄膜具有二维金属性传导，而体材料保持绝缘特性，证实了表面狄拉克态的存在。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究β-Ag2Te表面狄拉克态引起的涌现现象和设计基于异质结的器件结构提供了可行的平台。

Abstract: We report epitaxial growth of $β$-Ag2Te thin films by molecular beam epitaxy. $β$-Ag2Te, recently identified as a topological insulator, was grown by depositing Ag on InP substrate at room temperature followed by Te supply at elevated temperature. X-ray diffraction measurements and transmission electron microscopy analyses confirmed the (002) crystal orientation and the epitaxial atomic arrangement of $β$-Ag2Te thin films. Electrical transport measurements revealed that the $β$-Ag2Te thin film exhibits two-dimensional metallic conduction while the bulk remains insulating. The epitaxial $β$-Ag2Te thin films obtained here provide a viable platform for investigating emergent phenomena arising from surface Dirac states and for designing heterojunction-based device structures.

</details>


### [92] [Microstructural characteristics, atomic-scale features, and growth mechanisms of deuterides (hydrides) in hafnium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15420)
*Di Wang,Catriona M McGilvery,James O Douglas,Siyang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用多种显微技术表征了Hf合金中氘化物-基体界面的结构和化学特性，揭示了取向关系、位错分布、氧偏聚等关键信息，有助于理解氢化物-金属复合材料在核应用中的界面演化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铪氢化物是下一代核反应堆（特别是快裂变控制棒和聚变屏蔽）的有前景材料，但其固有的脆性促使其以氢化物-金属复合材料形式使用。这些复合材料的功能和力学性能强烈依赖于氢化物-基体界面的多尺度结构，因此需要深入理解界面特性。

Method: 采用多种显微技术组合：扫描电子显微镜（SEM）结合电子背散射衍射（EBSD）、透射电子显微镜（TEM）结合电子能量损失谱（EELS）、以及原子探针层析成像（APT）。研究对象为氘化处理的Hf合金，重点表征氘化物-基体界面。

Result: 表征了氘化物-基体界面的结构和化学特性，获得了关键信息：氘化物-基体晶体学取向关系、微观结构特征、失配诱导位错分布、电子能量损失特性，以及氘化物生长过程中的氧偏聚现象。

Conclusion: 这些发现有助于阐明界面演化机制，可能促进对氢化物-金属系统的更好理解，对核应用中这类材料的加工、性能和设计具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Hafnium hydride is a promising material for next-generation nuclear reactors, particularly as control rods for fast fission and shielding in fusion systems. The material's intrinsic brittleness encourages its use in the form of hydride-metal composites, where the functional and mechanical performance is strongly influenced by the multiscale structure of hydride-matrix interfaces. In this study, we employ a suite of microscopy techniques, including scanning electron microscopy with electron backscatter diffraction, transmission electron microscopy with electron energy-loss spectroscopy, and atom probe tomography, to investigate the deuteride-matrix interfaces in a deuterium-charged Hf alloy. We characterise their structure and chemistry, extracting key information including the deuteride-matrix crystallographic orientation relationship, microstructural features, misfit-induced dislocation distributions, electron energy-loss characteristics, and the segregation of oxygen during deuteride growth. These findings help clarify the mechanisms of interface evolution and may contribute to improved understanding of hydride-metal systems, with potential relevance for their processing, performance, and design in nuclear applications.

</details>


### [93] [Understanding early stages of low-temperature hydrogen-driven direct co-reduction of Fe-Ni mixed oxide thin films at the near atomic scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15456)
*Emmanuel Uwayezu,Shaolou Wei,Yujiao Li,Johannes D. Bartl,Dierk Raabe,Alfred Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了Fe50Ni50Ox薄膜在280°C纯H₂中的低温还原过程，揭示了还原早期阶段涉及NiFe₂O₄和NiO相分离为富Ni金属相和磁铁矿，最终形成近等原子FeNi合金的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多元多相氧化物的氢共还原动力学对于提高合金生产的可持续性和向氢经济转型至关重要，其优势包括降低能耗、增强动力学和氧化物向合金的转化。

Method: 使用Fe50Ni50Ox薄膜（包含NiFe₂O₄和NiO相）作为模型体系，在280°C纯H₂中进行不同时间的还原，通过原子探针层析（APT）等多种表征技术分析结构、形貌和纳米尺度变化。

Result: 低温还原为成核限制过程，具有超过5分钟的孕育期，晶界成核优先。APT显示还原早期阶段发生相分离，形成富Ni的FexNiy金属相和转化的剩余氧化物（磁铁矿Fe₃O₄）。进一步还原导致磁铁矿还原并合金化为近等原子FeNi合金。

Conclusion: 薄膜纳米结构和Ni的自催化效应（通过合金化和氢溢流）促进了低温还原和合金化，为直接从氧化物低温制备可调成分的Fe-Ni合金薄膜开辟了道路，并将氢直接还原多元氧化物的研究扩展到薄膜平台。

Abstract: Kinetic understanding of hydrogen co-reduction of multinary and multi-phase oxides is of interest for enhancing sustainability of alloy production and transition to a hydrogen-based economy. Benefits include decrease in energy consumption, enhanced kinetics, and conversion of oxides to alloys. Thin films provide a platform to study these processes as reactive co-deposition from multiple elemental, alloy or compound targets and precise oxygen flow control allow atomic mixing into various oxide phases which are well-defined nanoscale precursor structures for the subsequent reduction study at the near atomic scale. The early stages of hydrogen direct reduction of oxide thin films are investigated using a Fe50Ni50Ox thin film consisting of NiFe2O4 and NiO phases. After reduction at 280 C in pure H2 for different times, structural, morphological, and nanoscale changes were examined by different characterisation methods including atom probe tomography (APT). The low-temperature reduction is nucleation-limited marked by grain-boundary nucleation preceded by an incubation time of more than 5 min. APT revealed that the early-stages of the reduction involves phase separation into a Ni-rich FexNiy metallic phase and a transformed remaining oxide (magnetite, Fe3O4). Further reduction induces magnetite reduction and alloying into a nearly equiatomic FeNi alloy. The low-temperature reduction and alloying are facilitated by synergetic effects from the nanostructure of the film, and Ni autocatalytic effects through alloying and hydrogen spillover. The results pave the way for low-temperature formation of Fe-Ni alloy thin films with tunable compositions directly from oxides, and broaden the scope of hydrogen direct reduction of multinary oxides to thin-film platforms.

</details>


### [94] [High-Throughput Computational Exploration of MOFs for Short-Chain PFAS Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15503)
*Mengru Zhang,Satyanarayana Bonakala,Taku Watanabe,Karim Hamzaoui,Guillaume Maurin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发混合高通量计算筛选策略，结合经典力场和机器学习势能，从MOF数据库中筛选出对短链PFAS（PFBA）具有高选择性吸附性能的材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短链PFAS逐渐替代受监管的长链PFAS，但由于其高持久性、高迁移性以及对传统吸附剂亲和力弱，从水中去除仍然具有挑战性。需要开发高效吸附材料。

Method: 采用混合高通量计算筛选策略：1）从精选MOF数据集开始；2）使用基于UFF力场的蒙特卡洛模拟进行初步筛选；3）对初选MOFs使用通用机器学习势能（u-MLIP）重新评估，优化吸附预测并评估客体诱导的框架柔性；4）综合考虑PFBA亲和力、PFBA/水选择性、可持续性、水稳定性和合成可行性等实际因素。

Result: 成功筛选出四种高性能MOFs，这些材料在强PFBA相互作用、高PFBA/水选择性和实际可行性之间达到最佳平衡。验证了经典力场与u-MLIP结合可实现可扩展且定量准确的MOF吸附筛选。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了经典力场与机器学习势能相结合的方法能够实现可扩展、定量准确的MOF吸附筛选，并为针对短链PFAS的吸附剂理性设计建立了可转移的原则。

Abstract: Short-chain per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFASs) are increasingly replacing regulated long-chain PFASs, yet they remain challenging to remove from water due to their high persistence, mobility, and weak affinity toward conventional adsorbents. In this work, we developed a hybrid high-throughput computational screening (HTCS) strategy to identify high-performance MOFs for the selective adsorption of perfluorobutanoic acid (PFBA), a representative short-chain PFAS, from water. The workflow begins with a curated MOF dataset and employs Monte Carlo (MC) simulations based on synergistic use of a classical universal force field (UFF) and a universal machine-learned interatomic potential (u-MLIP), enabling scalable and quantitatively accurate prediction of adsorption across large MOF databases. A set of promising MOFs initially identified using UFF-based HTCS, that combine strong PFBA affinity and high PFBA/H2O selectivity were re-evaluated with u-MLIP to refine adsorption predictions and to assess guest-induced framework flexibility, enabling the exclusion of materials with unfavourable high water-framework interactions. Ultimately, four high-performance MOFs were identified that optimally balance strong PFBA interactions, high PFBA selectivity over water, and practical considerations including sustainability, water stability, and synthetic feasibility. This study demonstrates that combining classical force fields with u-MLIPs enables scalable, quantitatively accurate MOF adsorption screening and establishes transferable principles for the rational design of adsorbents targeting short-chain PFAS.

</details>


### [95] [Engineering van der Waals heterostructures for dispersion-selective meV-scale quantum sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15581)
*Elizabeth A. Peterson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出利用范德华异质结中的轨道杂化，基于色散关系选择性检测入射粒子，为meV尺度量子传感器提供新方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子传感方案难以区分目标粒子信号与声子/磁振子等本征激发，需要发展能基于色散关系选择性检测入射粒子的新技术

Method: 利用狄拉克材料范德华异质结的界面轨道杂化，通过应变和层数调控电子结构，实现仅对特定色散关系的粒子促进层间电荷转移

Result: 通过ZrTe5和HfTe5异质结的第一性原理DFT计算，证明了应变和层数可有效调控轨道杂化，为构建色散滤波器提供了原理验证

Conclusion: 狄拉克材料异质结可用于构建基于色散关系的粒子筛选器，为下一代meV尺度量子传感器开辟新途径

Abstract: Quantum sensing of meV-scale scattering and absorption of impinging particles with electrons in solid state detectors is a challenging technological advancement with the potential to enable breakthroughs in quantum information applications and studies of fundamental physics. However, a key obstacle for current sensing schemes is the difficulty in distinguishing the signals from particles of interest and from intrinsic excitations, like phonons or magnons. Here we propose a technique to selectively detect impinging particles based not only on their imparted energy, but specifically by their dispersion relations. By harnessing interfacial orbital hybridization in van der Waals heterostructures of Dirac materials, interlayer charge transfer may be promoted only for pre-selected impinging particles of interest. Using first-principles density functional theory (DFT) calculations of heterostructures of the layered Dirac materials ZrTe5 and HfTe5, we examine the effects of strain and layer number for successfully tuning orbital hybridization in their electronic structure. We demonstrate a proof-or-principle feasibility study for using Dirac materials to construct dispersion filters to be leveraged for next-generation meV-scale quantum sensors.

</details>
