### [96] [Hierarchical structure of primary and hybridization-induced superconducting correlations in bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13604)
*Hiroshi Watanabe,Hirofumi Sakakibara,Kazuhiko Kuroki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过变分蒙特卡洛方法揭示双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇中s±超导配对的分层结构：主要配对源于Ni 3d_z²轨道的键合-反键合分裂，轨道杂化将超导关联重新分布到d_x²-y²通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇中接近80K的超导转变温度引发了关于其微观起源的激烈争论。虽然s±能隙对称性被广泛提出，但负责配对的电子自由度仍未确定。需要理解轨道自由度在多层关联超导体中的独特作用。

Method: 使用变分蒙特卡洛方法研究双层双轨道Hubbard模型，分析轨道杂化和键合-反键合分裂对超导配对的影响。

Result: 发现分层配对结构：主要配对相互作用源于Ni 3d_z²轨道的键合-反键合分裂，而轨道杂化将超导关联重新分布到d_x²-y²通道，尽管其本征配对相互作用较弱。这种配对起源与超导关联的区分解释了为什么两个轨道通道表现出可比的长程关联。所得的s±态对费米面拓扑变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果调和了看似竞争的理论方案，提供了对双层镍酸盐超导性的全面理解，强调了轨道杂化在多层关联超导体中的独特作用。配对主要源于d_z²轨道，但轨道杂化使d_x²-y²通道也表现出显著的超导关联。

Abstract: High-pressure superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, with a transition temperature approaching 80 K, has stimulated intense debate regarding its microscopic origin. Although an $s_{\pm}$ gap symmetry has been widely proposed, the electronic degrees of freedom responsible for pairing remain unsettled. Here we investigate a bilayer two-orbital Hubbard model using the variational Monte Carlo method and reveal a hierarchical pairing structure in bilayer nickelates. The primary pairing interaction originates from the bonding--antibonding splitting of the Ni $3d_{z^2}$ orbitals, while orbital hybridization redistributes superconducting correlations to the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ channel despite its weak intrinsic pairing interaction. This distinction between the origin of pairing and resulting superconducting correlations explains why the two orbital channels exhibit comparable long-range correlations. The resulting $s_{\pm}$ state is robust against changes in Fermi-surface topology. These results reconcile apparently competing theoretical scenarios and provide a comprehensive understanding, highlighting the distinctive role of orbital hybridization in multilayer correlated superconductors.

</details>


### [97] [Superhydrides on the way to ambient pressure: weak localization and persistent X-ray photoconductivity in BaSiH$_{8}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14051)
*Dmitrii V. Semenok,Di Zhou,Sven Luther,Toni Helm,Hirokazu Kadobayashi,Yuki Nakamoto,Katsuya Shimizu,Kirill S. Pervakov,Andrei V. Sadakov,Oleg A. Sobolevskiy,Vladimir M. Pudalov,Simone Di Cataldo,Roman Lucrezi,Lilia Boeri,Michele Galasso,Frederico G. Alabarse,Ivan A. Troyan,Viktor V. Struzhkin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Ba-Si-H体系中合成了立方BaSiH₈，该超氢化物在18-31 GPa压力下合成后可在常压下保持稳定，表现出从高压超导到低压半导体/弱金属的转变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低超氢化物的稳定压力是氢饱和化合物化学中最关键的挑战之一，旨在实现常压稳定的氢存储材料。

Method: 在0-142 GPa压力范围和4-317 K温度范围内，使用输运测量、¹H核磁共振、单晶和粉末X射线衍射研究Ba-Si-H体系，合成立方BaSiH₈。

Result: 在18-31 GPa压力下成功合成立方BaSiH₈，该化合物可在减压至常压条件下保持稳定并能从金刚石压砧中回收。在142 GPa下表现出金属性和超导性（T_c=9 K，B_c2(0)=13-16 T），但在50 GPa以下表现为简并半导体（带隙<0.4 meV）或弱金属，具有弱电子局域化、负磁阻、光伏效应和X射线/可见光范围内的持续光电导性。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了高压合成后常压稳定的Ba-Si多氢化物，克服了超氢化物化学中的关键瓶颈，为氢存储的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reducing the stabilization pressure of superhydrides represents one of the most important challenges in hydrogen-saturated compound chemistry. Moving in this direction, we studied the Ba-Si-H system at 0-142 GPa using transport measurements, 1H nuclear magnetic resonance, single-crystal and powder X-ray diffraction in the temperature range of 4-317 K. We synthesized the previously predicted cubic BaSiH$_{8}$ at pressures of 18-31 GPa. Remarkably, we demonstrate that BaSiH$_8$ remains stable upon decompression to ambient conditions and can be recovered from the diamond anvil cell. Obtained Ba-Si polyhydrides exhibit metallic and superconducting properties ($\textit{T$_c$}$ = 9 K, $\textit{B$_{c2}$}$(0)=13-16 T) at 142 GPa. However, at pressures below 50 GPa, these hydrides behave as degenerate semiconductors (bandgap < 0.4 meV) or poor metals with weak electron localization, negative magnetoresistance, photovoltaic effect, and persistent photoconductivity in the X-ray and visible range. Our work demonstrates the high-pressure synthesis of Ba-Si polyhydrides that remain stable upon decompression to ambient conditions, overcoming a critical bottleneck in superhydride chemistry and establishing a foundation for practical applications in hydrogen storage.

</details>


### [98] [The stripe state at 1/8 Ba doping hosts optimal superconductivity in La-214 cuprates under low in-plane stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14108)
*V. Sazgari,S. S. Islam,M. Lamotte,J. N. Graham,O. Gerguri,P. Kràl,I. Maetsu,T. Shiroka,G. Simutis,R. Khasanov,R. Sarkar,A. Steppke,N. A. Shepelin,M. Müller,M. Bartkowiak,M. Janoschek,J. Chang,H. H. Klauss,T. Adachi,G. D. Gu,J. M. Tranquada,H. Luetkens,Z. Guguchia*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: LBCO-0.125在面内单轴应力下表现出超导转变温度的巨幅提升（从5K到37K），同时自旋条纹序的磁体积分数显著减小，LTT相相关的电阻峰被完全抑制，表明静态条纹序主要与超导相位相干性竞争而非配对强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究La₂₋ₓBaₓCuO₄（LBCO）体系在1/8掺杂异常附近的对称选择性晶格调控效应，特别是x=0.125这一尚未充分探索的组分在单轴应力下的响应，以理解条纹序与超导性之间的竞争关系。

Method: 结合μ子自旋旋转（μSR）、交流磁化率和电阻率测量，研究LBCO-0.125在45°方向施加面内单轴应力下的超导性、自旋条纹序和结构响应；同时对x=0.115和0.135进行补充电阻率测量以追踪异常两侧的演化。

Result: LBCO-0.125在0.5 GPa应力下，体超导转变温度从5K大幅提升至37K（零电阻态），超导转变起始温度高达46K；自旋条纹序起始温度略有下降，但磁体积分数减少约一半；所有掺杂下的LTT相相关电阻峰完全被抑制。

Conclusion: LTT相的抑制和静态自旋条纹序体积分数的减少是发展最优三维超导性的关键；条纹相关相互作用增强配对强度，而静态条纹序主要与超导的相位相干性竞争，而非配对本身，这解释了x=0.125在应力下获得最高Tc的现象。

Abstract: The cuprate system La$_{2-x}$Ba$_{x}$CuO$_{4}$ (LBCO) exhibits a pronounced sensitivity to in-plane uniaxial stress, particularly near the 1/8 doping anomaly, where stripe order strongly suppresses bulk superconductivity. While previous studies have focused on compositions close to 0.125, the commensurate $x$=0.125 phase remains largely unexplored under symmetry-selective lattice tuning. Here, we combine muon-spin rotation ($μ$SR), AC susceptibility, and electrical resistivity to investigate superconductivity, spin-stripe order, and structural response in LBCO-0.125 under in-plane uniaxial stress applied 45$^\circ$ to the Cu-O bond direction. Complementary resistivity measurements on $x$=0.115 and 0.135 track the evolution across both sides of the anomaly. We observe a giant enhancement of the bulk superconducting transition temperature in LBCO-0.125, increasing from 5 K to 37 K under 0.5 GPa. While the onset temperature of spin-stripe order decreases only modestly, the magnetic volume fraction is reduced by about a factor of two, with local order preserved. Simultaneously, the resistivity peak associated with the LTT phase is fully suppressed across all dopings. These results demonstrate that suppression of the LTT phase and reduction of the static spin-stripe-ordered volume fraction are crucial for the development of optimal three-dimensional superconductivity. Strikingly, the composition $x$=0.125, with the most robust stripe stability and the lowest ambient-pressure $T_{\rm c}$ develops the highest $T_{\rm c}$ under stress, reaching a zero-resistance state at 37 K and an onset of the superconducting transition as high as 46 K. This behavior indicates that stripe-related interactions enhance pairing strength, while static stripe order competes with superconductivity primarily at the level of phase coherence rather than pairing itself.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Interference Breaks Bias Symmetry at Extended Superconducting Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14329)
*Vishal Tripathi,Goutam Sheet*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 粒子-空穴对称性不保证超导界面偏压对称输运，有限尺寸界面量子干涉导致本征偏压不对称电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量的粒子-空穴对称性强制超导界面偏压对称输运，但作者发现有限空间扩展的界面中量子干涉会破坏这种对称性。

Method: 采用保持精确粒子-空穴对称性的紧束缚散射形式，分析电子和空穴在扩展界面传播中积累不等相位，导致本征偏压不对称电导。

Result: 界面作为有效Andreev干涉仪，相干多重反射产生特征阻尼振荡；不对称性源于正常态干涉，但偏压依赖性由超导能隙决定，可作为尖锐交叉尺度被清晰分辨。

Conclusion: 偏压不对称性可作为干涉测量学探针，用于探测非局域界面物理和超导能标，在扩展界面不可避免的混合和拓扑系统中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Particle-hole symmetry of the Bogoliubov-de~Gennes Hamiltonian is widely assumed to enforce bias-symmetric transport at superconducting interfaces. We show that this expectation fails generically for interfaces with finite spatial extent due to quantum interference. Using a tight-binding scattering formalism that preserves exact particle-hole symmetry, we demonstrate that propagation through an extended interface causes electrons and holes to accumulate unequal phases, leading to intrinsic bias-asymmetric conductance. The interface thereby acts as an effective Andreev interferometer with characteristic damped oscillations arising from coherent multiple reflections within the barrier. While the asymmetry originates from normal-state interference, its bias dependence is governed by the superconducting gap, which emerges as a sharp crossover scale that can be clearly resolved even when conventional coherence peaks are weak or absent. Thus we present bias asymmetry as an interferometric, spectroscopic probe of nonlocal interface physics and superconducting energy scales in hybrid and topological systems where extended interfaces are unavoidable.

</details>


### [100] [A Unified Understanding of the Experimental Controlling of the T$_\text{c}$ of Bilayer Nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14519)
*Zeyu Chen,Jia-Heng Ji,Yu-Bo Liu,Ming Zhang,Fan Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于单轨道双层t-J模型，统一解释了La₃Ni₂O₇超导转变温度在不同实验条件下的变化规律，发现J⊥的变化是主要调控机制，并提出电子掺杂和增强压应变可进一步提高Tc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期一系列实验通过不同环境条件（Sm/Nd替代、压力、压应变、空穴掺杂）调控双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇的超导转变温度Tc，引起了广泛关注。需要建立一个统一的理论框架来解释这些看似分散的实验现象。

Method: 采用先前提出的最小化单d_{x²-y²}轨道双层t-J∥-J⊥模型，结合密度泛函理论计算获取不同实验条件下的模型参数，使用slave-boson平均场和密度矩阵重整化群方法求解模型，并与弱耦合随机相位近似方法进行对比。

Result: 理论结果与实验定性一致：1）Sm/Nd替代比例增加时，压力下的体材料Tc增强；2）体材料Tc-压力关系呈穹顶形曲线；3）薄膜Tc随压应变增强而提高；4）空穴掺杂导致Tc下降。这些变化主要源于层间交换耦合J⊥随实验条件的变化。

Conclusion: 强耦合t-J∥-J⊥模型为双层镍酸盐的超导调控提供了自然的理论解释，层间耦合J⊥是调控Tc的关键参数。提出通过高价元素替代实现电子掺杂或进一步增强薄膜压应变可提高Tc，为实验设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recently, a series of experiments which control the T$_\text{c}$ of the bilayer nickelates La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ through varying environmental conditions, including the rare-earth Sm/Nd substitution, the pressure on the bulk material, the compressive strain on the film and the hole doping through over-oxidation or alkaline earth element substitution have caught great interests. Here, we provide a unified understanding toward all these experiments based on the minimal single $d_{x^2-y^2}$-orbital bilayer $t-J_\parallel-J_\perp$ model proposed previously. With model parameters input from density-functional-theoretical calculations under varying experimental conditions, we adopt combined slave-boson-mean-field and density-matrix-renormalization-group approaches to solve the model and compare with experiments. Our results yield that, the bulk T$_\text{c}$ under pressure enhances with the Sm/Nd substitution fraction, the bulk T$_\text{c}$-pressure relation takes a dome shaped curve, the T$_\text{c}$ of the thin film enhances with compressive strain. The obtained parameters dependence of T$_\text{c}$ in these three experiments mainly originates from the variation of $J_\perp$ with experimental conditions. As for the hole doping, our results provide that T$_\text{c}$ decreases with the hole doping level $δ$, due to reduced density of state for the $d_{x^2-y^2}$-orbital. All these results are qualitatively consistent with experiments. We further conduct a comparative weak-coupling random-phase-approximation (RPA) based study on these experiments and find that our strong-coupling $t-J_\parallel-J_\perp$ model provides a more natural understanding of the experiments. We propose that electron doping implemented through substitution of La by element with higher valence, or further enhancement of the compressive strain in the film, can enhance T$_\text{c}$.

</details>


### [101] [Probing the Meissner effect in single crystals of $\mathbf{Bi_2Sr_2Ca_2Cu_3O_{10+δ}}$ via wide-field quantum microscopy under high pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15102)
*Masahiro Ohkuma,Ryo Matsumoto,Shintaro Adachi,Shinobu Onoda,Takao Watanabe,Kenji Ohta,Yoshihiko Takano,Keigo Arai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过不同传压介质（cBN和KBr）研究Bi-2223超导体Tc的压力依赖性，发现介质对超导转变有显著影响：KBr中70K抗磁响应保持至23GPa，而cBN中11GPa以上消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前高压研究中关于Bi-2223超导转变温度压力依赖性的矛盾结果：流体介质中报道Tc回升，固体介质中则出现绝缘化转变。缺乏不同传压介质的直接对比研究。

Method: 采用基于氮空位中心的宽场量子显微镜技术，在高压下探测磁响应。使用两种不同传压介质：立方氮化硼(cBN)和溴化钾(KBr)，进行对比实验。

Result: 在KBr介质中，70K附近的抗磁响应（指示超导转变）保持至23GPa；而在cBN介质中，该响应在11GPa以上和70K时消失。表明Bi-2223对压力环境高度敏感。

Conclusion: Bi-2223超导体对压力环境极为敏感，传压介质的选择显著影响高压下超导行为。研究强调了静水压条件在铜氧化物超导体研究中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure dependence of the superconducting transition temperature ($T_{\rm c}$) in optimally doped Bi$_2$Sr$_2$Ca$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{10+δ}$ (Bi-2223) single crystals using different pressure-transmitting media. Previous high-pressure studies have reported conflicting behaviors, ranging from a resurgence of $T_{\rm c}$ of optimally doped Bi-2223 in fluid media to an insulating-like transition in solid media. However, a direct comparison of the effects of different pressure-transmitting media is lacking. Here, we employed wide-field quantum microscopy based on nitrogen-vacancy centers to probe the magnetic response under high pressure, utilizing cBN and KBr as media. We observed that a diamagnetic response near 70 K, indicative of the superconducting transition, persisted up to 23 GPa in KBr, whereas it disappeared above 11 GPa and 70 K in cBN. These results demonstrate the high sensitivity of Bi-2223 to the pressure environment and highlight the critical role of hydrostatic pressure in cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [102] [Hidden Zeeman Field in Odd-Parity Magnets: An Ideal Platform for Topological Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15147)
*Xun-Jiang Luo,Zi-Ting Sun,Xilin Feng,Mingliang Tian,K. T. Law*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文揭示了奇宇称磁体（OPMs）中隐藏的塞曼场效应，修正了对其基本物理的理解，并证明OPMs是实现无外场鲁棒拓扑超导体的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管奇宇称磁体（OPMs）作为非常规磁性的基本类别引起了广泛关注，但对其基本理解仍存在严重缺陷。先前研究仅关注非相对论性自旋劈裂（NSS），而忽略了磁性有序固有的时间反演对称性破缺。本文旨在揭示OPMs中隐藏的塞曼场效应，并探索其在拓扑超导体中的应用潜力。

Method: 通过解析的f波磁体模型，证明NSS微观上源于一个涌现的规范场，表现为实空间的自旋环流序。利用大尺度NSS（eV量级）使常规超导性与隐藏塞曼场稳健共存，并基于OPMs设计了一系列具有不同马约拉纳边界模式的拓扑超导体。

Result: 揭示了OPMs普遍存在源于时间反演对称性破缺的隐藏塞曼场，该场从根本上重塑了能带结构。大尺度NSS（eV量级）使常规超导性与隐藏塞曼场（塞曼劈裂达数百meV）稳健共存。基于OPMs成功设计出支持大拓扑区域的拓扑超导体，包括具有单向马约拉纳边缘态的系统。

Conclusion: 本文修正了关于奇宇称磁体的一个基本误解，揭示了其隐藏的塞曼场效应。OPMs被确立为实现无外场、鲁棒拓扑超导体的多功能平台，为拓扑量子计算等应用提供了新的材料基础。

Abstract: Odd-parity magnets (OPMs) have emerged as a fundamental class of unconventional magnetisms, characterized by time-reversal-preserving non-relativistic spin splitting (NSS). Despite growing interest, the fundamental understanding of OPMs remains critically incomplete, as previous studies have focused exclusively on NSS while overlooking the intrinsically broken time-reversal symmetry ($\mathcal{T}$) inherent to magnetic order. In this work, we reveal that OPMs universally host a hidden Zeeman field rooted in this $\mathcal{T}$-breaking, which fundamentally reshapes their band structure. Through an analytical $f$-wave magnet model, we show that NSS microscopically originates from an emergent gauge field, manifesting as a real-space spin loop current order. Crucially, the large NSS (eV scale) enables conventional superconductivity to coexist robustly with the hidden Zeeman field, with Zeeman splitting reaches hundreds of meV. This unique band structure establishes OPMs as an ideal platform for topological superconductors (TSCs), supporting large topological regions. Based on OPMs, we engineer a series of TSCs hosting distinct Majorana boundary modes, including unidirectional Majorana edge states. Our work corrects a fundamental misconception about OPMs and establishes them as a versatile platform for field-free and robust TSCs.

</details>


### [103] [Quench Protection in Insulated REBCO Conductors: Design Optimization and Fast Detection via REBCO SQD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15244)
*Hajar Zgour,Walid Abdel Maksoud,Bertrand Baudouy,Antoine Guinet*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 比较两种REBCO超导导体失超保护方法：铜稳定层优化与共绕REBCO失超探测器，通过THEA建模验证两者均能有效控制热点温度并实现早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在聚变能应用中，绝缘REBCO导体在失超时面临保护挑战：正常区传播缓慢且验证延迟导致热点温度可达破坏性水平，需要可靠保护方案。

Method: 使用一维THEA建模比较两种保护策略：(1)优化铜稳定层横截面积以改善热管理；(2)采用共绕REBCO失超探测器（通过脱氧降低Tc和Ic以实现早期转变），该探测器电气隔离但热耦合。

Result: 合适的稳定层横截面选择可使失超期间热点温度保持在安全限值内；REBCO失超探测器能在更低温度下实现失超检测，提供更早的保护响应。

Conclusion: 铜稳定层优化和REBCO失超探测器均为有效的REBCO导体失超保护方案，前者通过热管理控制温度，后者通过早期检测提升保护可靠性。

Abstract: This work was conducted within the framework of the exploratory French project PEPR SupraFusion, which aims to advance the field of fusion energy by developing High-Temperature Superconductor (HTS)-based demonstrators capable of storing significant energy while operating under high magnetic fields and currents. Ensuring a reliable protection during a quench in Insulated REBCO conductors is challenging\,: slow normal-zone propagation and validation delays allow the hotspot's temperature to reach damaging levels. We compare (i) conductor protection via copper-stabilizer optimization and (ii) a co-wound, REBCO superconducting quench detector (SQD) that is electrically isolated yet thermally coupled and intentionally deoxygenated to lower Tc and Ic for an earlier transition. Onedimensional THEA modeling shows that a good choice of stabilizer cross-section makes the protection possible during quench events by keeping the temperature of the hotspot within a safe limit. The simulations also demonstrate that the use of a REBCO SQD enables the quench detection at lower temperatures.

</details>


### [104] [First-principles prediction of high-temperature superconductivity in stretched carbon nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15305)
*Hua-Zhen Li,Xun-Wang Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 碳纳米管在4.5%单轴拉伸应变下可实现162K的高温超导，远高于未应变状态，主要归因于声子软化、强电声耦合和费米能级电子态密度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准一维体系的超导性是一个重要但被低估的研究领域，本文旨在探索碳纳米管在单轴拉伸应变下的超导特性，特别是能否实现高温超导。

Method: 基于电声耦合机制，系统研究准一维碳纳米管在单轴拉伸应变下的超导性，计算超导临界温度并分析其物理机制。

Result: 在4.5%单轴拉伸应变下，超导临界温度达到峰值162K，远高于未应变碳纳米管。声子整体软化、强电声耦合以及费米能级电子态密度增加是实现高温超导的关键因素。

Conclusion: 拉伸是调控一维材料超导性的有效方法，更重要的是表明碳纳米管中可能出现高温超导现象，为准一维超导研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Superconductivity in quasi-one-dimensional systems is an significant but undervalued research field. In this work, based on the electron-phonon coupling mechanism, we systematically investigate the superconductivity in quasi-one-dimensional carbon nanotube under uniaxial tensile strain. The calculated superconducting critical temperature attains its peak value of 162 K at a uniaxial tensile strain of 4.5\%, being drastically higher than the counterpart in the unstrained carbon nanotube. An overall softening of phonons, strong electron-phonon coupling, and an increase of electronic density of states at the Fermi level, play key roles in achieving high-temperature superconductivity in this system. Our research demonstrates that stretching is an effective approach to modulating the superconductivity one-dimensional materials, and more importantly, indicates that high-temperature superconductivity may occur in carbon nanotubes.

</details>


### [105] [Effect of pulse duration on current-induced selective oxygen migration in high-Tc superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15509)
*Fridrich Egyenes,Daniel Stoffels,Stefan Marinkovic,Bernd Aichner,Huidong Li,Anna Palau,Milan Tapajna,Wolfgang Lang,Alejandro V. Silhanek*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究探索了脉冲宽度对YBCO薄膜中电迁移起始电流的影响，发现当脉冲时间缩短至10μs以下时，电迁移起始电流迅速增加，表明过程变得更加非热主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于YBCO中电迁移的研究主要局限于毫秒或更长时间尺度的脉冲激励，其中热效应显著影响过程。本研究旨在探索脉冲宽度对电迁移起始电流的影响，特别是在更短时间尺度下，以区分热效应和非热效应的贡献。

Method: 通过实验测量YBCO桥结构中电迁移起始电流I_EM与脉冲宽度δt的关系，覆盖200 ns到1 ms的范围。结合解析和数值方法估算局部温度变化，分析热效应在电迁移过程中的作用。

Result: 当脉冲宽度δt减小到约10μs以下时，电迁移起始电流I_EM迅速增加。温度估算表明，随着脉冲缩短，局部温度降低，电迁移过程变得更加非热主导。

Conclusion: 短脉冲下电迁移起始电流的增加表明过程从热主导转变为非热主导。这一发现对忆阻器操作和薄膜中热磁不稳定性的描述具有重要意义。

Abstract: High current densities can induce the directional diffusion of atoms in metallic films. In YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-δ}$ (YBCO), this electromigration process selectively acts on oxygen atoms lying in the Cu-O chains, permitting to vary the oxygen concentration in a targeted spot of high current density. This approach has proven successful in mapping the phase diagram of the material as a function of carrier concentration or as a way to manufacture memristive devices owing to its reversibility under small bipolar excitations. Thus far, most of the investigations have been limited to pulsed excitation with current/voltage pulses on a millisecond or longer scale, for which thermal effects undeniably influence the process. In the present work, we explore the impact of pulse length $δt$ on the onset current of electromigration, $I_{\text{EM}}$, of YBCO bridges, covering the range from 200 ns to 1 ms. As $δt$ decreases below $\sim 10~μ$s, $I_{\text{EM}}$ exhibits a rapid increase. Analytical and numerical estimates of the local temperature show that as pulses shorten, the temperature decreases, making the electromigration process more athermal. These findings are relevant for the operation of memristors and should be taken into account when describing the effects of thermomagnetic instabilities in thin films.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Adaptive tensor train metadynamics for high-dimensional free energy exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13549)
*Nils E. Strand,Siyao Yang,Yuehaw Khoo,Aaron R. Dinner*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: TT-Metadynamics：通过张量列车压缩技术解决高维CV自由能面采样问题，实现计算成本随CV数量线性增长而非指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学模拟中，高效探索高维集体变量（CV）自由能面是关键挑战。传统方法如元动力学在超过少数CV时计算成本呈指数增长，限制了其在多变量系统中的应用。

Method: 提出TT-Metadynamics方法：将元动力学中累积的高斯函数和定期压缩为低秩张量列车（TT）表示。开发"sketching"算法实现CV数量的线性缩放，避免偏置势评估成本随模拟时间增长。

Result: 在最多14个CV的基准系统中，TT-Metadynamics的精度匹配或超过标准元动力学，特别是在高能垒系统中表现更优。实现了内存高效使用和计算成本线性增长。

Conclusion: TT-Metadynamics为计算多CV自由能提供了一种可扩展且有效的方法，解决了高维采样中的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: A key challenge for molecular dynamics simulations is efficient exploration of free energy landscapes over relevant collective variables (CV). Common methods for enhancing sampling become prohibitively inefficient beyond only a few CVs; in the case of the widely-used metadynamics method, the computational cost of evaluating and storing the bias potential grows exponentially with the number of dimensions. Here, we introduce TT-Metadynamics, in which the accumulated sum of Gaussian functions in the original metadynamics method is periodically compressed into a low-rank tensor train (TT) representation. The TT enables efficient memory use and prevents the computational cost of evaluating the bias potential from increasing with simulation time. We present a "sketching" algorithm that allows us to construct the TT with linear scaling in the number of CVs. Applied to benchmark systems with up to 14 CVs, the accuracy of TT-Metadynamics matches or exceeds that of standard metadynamics in long simulations, particularly in systems with high barriers. These results establish TT-Metadynamics as a scalable and effective method for computing free energies that are functions of several CVs.

</details>


### [107] [A Primary Unified Geometric Framework of Molecular Reaction Dynamics Based on the Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13923)
*Xingyu Zhang,Jinke Yu,Qingyong Meng*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于变分原理的分子反应动力学几何框架，统一电子结构和量子动力学，引入几何相位，并探讨优化视角下的理论扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要求解薛定谔方程才能"看到"反应过程，本文旨在建立一个基于变分原理的几何框架，统一描述分子反应动力学，并自然引入几何相位概念。

Method: 1) 数学预备：最小作用量原理和山路定理；2) 物理预备：等效原理推导动能算符，AI技术构建势能面，简化弯曲时空中的电磁相互作用；3) 单粒子近似作为变分求解薛定谔方程的强有力ansatz；4) 几何描述统一电子结构和量子动力学变分方法。

Result: 建立了非零曲率时空中的核哈密顿量，表明可通过曲率引入规范场（如锥形交叉附近的核动能算符附加项），自然引入几何相位，并基于优化特性扩展了理论讨论。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为分子反应动力学提供了统一描述，将变分原理、几何相位和优化理论相结合，为理解化学反应过程开辟了新途径，并可通过AI技术和优化视角进一步扩展。

Abstract: This work describes a geometric framework on molecular reaction dynamics based on the variational principle, where the Schr{ö}dinger equation must be solved to ``see'' how a reaction occurs. First, the mathematical preliminaries are given by discussing the principle of least action and the mountain pass theorem. Second, we discuss the physical preliminaries, including the principle of equivalence for deriving the kinetic energy operator (KEO) and artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to build the potential energy surface (PES) in general spacetime. Moreover, we simplified electromagnetic interactions in curved spacetime within the molecular system and consequently, we are able to construct the nuclear Hamiltonian in nonzero curvature spacetime. This indicates possibility to introduce gauge fields through the curvature, such as additional term in the nuclear KEO near a conical intersection. Third, the single-particle approximation provides a powful ansatz to solve the Schr{ö}dinger equation by variational principle. Thus, one can formulate the variational approaches for either electronic structure or quantum dynamics. In this work, based on previous discussions ({\it Phys. Chem. Chem. Phys.} {\bf 27} (2025), 20397) we unified them by a geometric description, where the geometric phase is naturally introduced. Finally, due to optimization characteristic of the present theory, further discussions on the present theory from optimization insight are also given, including two postulates, generative AI techniques, role of perturbation, and Markov process in optimization.

</details>


### [108] [Systematically Improvable Numerical Atomic Orbital Basis Using Contracted Truncated Spherical Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13995)
*Yike Huang,Zuxin Jin,Linfeng Zhang,Mohan Chen,Rui Chen,Ling Li*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种通过截断球面波收缩构建数值原子轨道基组的新方案，用于密度泛函理论中的Kohn-Sham方程求解，该方案在保持系统可改进性的同时提高了基组的可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密度泛函理论框架下求解Kohn-Sham方程需要高质量的数值原子轨道基组。现有方案存在局限性，特别是在描述导带和实现良好可转移性方面需要改进。

Method: 开发了一种通过截断球面波收缩构建数值原子轨道基组的方案。该方案通过最小化剩余空间中动能算符的迹来收缩轨道，推广了原有的溢出最小化方案。使用截断球面波而非平面波作为展开基，能更有效地桥接参考态与数值原子轨道，并消除周期性镜像间的虚假相互作用。

Result: 基准测试表明，构建的数值原子轨道基组在分子和体相系统的多种性质上均达到满意精度，包括总能、键长、原子化能、晶格常数、内聚能、带隙和能级对齐。通过包含未占据态，在描述导带方面的改进可转移性被证明是有效且显著的。

Conclusion: 提出的截断球面波收缩方案成功构建了高质量数值原子轨道基组，在保持系统可改进性的同时显著提高了基组的可转移性，特别是在描述导带方面表现优异。

Abstract: To solve the Kohn-Sham equation within the framework of density functional theory, we develop a scheme to construct numerical atomic orbital (NAO) basis sets by contracting truncated spherical waves (TSWs). The contraction minimizes the trace of the kinetic operator in the residual space, generalizing the spillage minimizing scheme [M. Chen et al., J. Phys. Condens. Matter 22, 445501 (2010); P. Lin et al., Phys. Rev. B 103, 235131 (2021)]. In addition to the systematic improvability inherited from previous schemes, the use of TSW instead of plane waves as the expansion basis bridges reference states and NAOs more effectively, and eliminates spurious interactions between periodic images, thereby enabling better transferability through the inclusion of extensive reference states. Benchmarks demonstrate that the constructed NAO achieves satisfactory precision for various properties of both molecules and bulk systems, including total energy, bond length, atomization energy, lattice constant, cohesive energy, band gap, and energy-level alignment. By incorporating unoccupied states, the improved transferability in describing the conduction band is demonstrated to be effective and substantial.

</details>


### [109] [Nonadiabatic rare events from transition-path sampling of MASH trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14102)
*Danial Ghamari,Jeremy O. Richardson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 将MASH（映射表面跳跃）与过渡路径采样结合，为稀有非绝热反应提供高效模拟框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有非绝热反应在许多重要分子过程中至关重要，但直接动力学模拟难以捕捉这些事件

Method: 结合MASH（映射表面跳跃）与过渡路径采样，利用MASH轨迹的马尔可夫性、时间可逆性和刘维尔定理保持性

Result: 成功应用于自旋-玻色子模型，在广泛的非绝热耦合强度范围内研究热平衡态，能够详细分析反应机制和统计/动力学性质

Conclusion: 该方法为研究稀有非绝热过程提供了实用系统工具，可超越暴力模拟的限制

Abstract: Rare nonadiabatic reactions are a key component of many important molecular processes but are challenging to capture with direct dynamical simulations. In this paper, we combine our recently developed mapping approach to surface hopping (MASH) with transition-path sampling to create a framework to efficiently simulate these rare events. This is possible because MASH trajectories are Markovian, time-reversible and obey Liouville's theorem. The combined approach generates nonadiabatic reactive pathways without biasing the underlying dynamics. The resulting ensemble allows for a detailed analysis of reaction mechanisms and the unraveling of statistical and dynamical properties, including rate constants. We apply the method to study a spin-boson model in thermal equilibrium over a wide range of diabatic coupling strengths. Our results demonstrate how this approach provides a practical and systematic tool for investigating rare nonadiabatic processes, potentially beyond the reach of brute-force simulations.

</details>


### [110] [The Python Simulations of Chemistry Framework: 10 years of an open-source quantum chemistry project](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14155)
*Qiming Sun,Matthew R Hermes,Xiaojie Wu,Huanchen Zhai,Xing Zhang,Abdelrahman M. Ahmed,Juan José Aucar,Oliver J. Backhouse,Samragni Banerjee,Peng Bao,Nikolay A. Bogdanov,Kyle Bystrom,Frédéric Chapoton,Ning-Yuan Chen,Ivan Yu. Chernyshov,Helen S. Clifford,Sander Cohen-Janes,Zhi-Hao Cui,Nike Dattani,Linus Bjarne Dittmer,Sebastian Ehlert,Janus Juul Eriksen,Francesco A. Evangelista,Simon A. Ewing,Ardavan Farahvash,Kevin Focke,Yang Gao,Kevin E. Gasperich,Nathan Gillispie,Jonas Greiner,Matthew R. Hennefarth,Jan Hermann,Christopher Hillenbrand,Joonatan Huhtasalo,Basil Ibrahim,Bhavnesh Jangid,Alireza Nejati Javaremi,Andrew J. Jenkins,Yu Jin,Daniel S. King,Derk Pieter Kooi,Henrik R. Larsson,Bryan Tak Gwong Lau,Seunghoon Lee,Susi Lehtola,Chenghan Li,Hao Li,Jiachen Li,Rui Li,Shuhang Li,Aleksandr O. Lykhin,Nastasia Mauger,Pablo del Mazo-Sevillano,Jonathan Moussa,Kousuke Nakano,Verena A. Neufeld,Linqing Peng,Hung Q. Pham,Peter Pinski,Pavel Pokhilko,Zhichen Pu,Yubing Qian,Stephen Jon Quiton,Wanja T. Schulze,Thais R. Scott,Aniruddha Seal,James E. T. Smith,Kori E. Smyser,Terrence Stahl,Chong Sun,Kevin J. Sung,Egor Trushin,Shiv Upadhyay,Ethan A. Vo,Thijs Vogels,Shirong Wang,Tai Wang,Xiao Wang,Xubo Wang,Yuanheng Wang,Mark Williamson,Junjie Yang,Hong-Zhou Ye,Chia-Nan Yeh,Haiyang Yu,Jincheng Yu,Victor Wen-zhe Yu,Chaoqun Zhang,Dayou Zhang,Zijun Zhao,Zehao Zhou,Andrew J. Zhu,Tianyu Zhu,Timothy C. Berkelbach,Laura Gagliardi,Sandeep Sharma,Alexander Sokolov,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: PySCF 2020-2024进展综述：新增模块、方法学、基础设施改进与性能基准测试


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PySCF作为广泛使用的开源电子结构理论平台，自2020年综述以来经历了显著发展，需要系统总结新进展以指导用户和开发者

Method: 通过文献综述方式，系统梳理PySCF在过去四年中的主要发展，包括新模块、方法学创新、基础设施变更和性能基准测试

Result: 全面总结了PySCF在量子化学方法开发、计算效率、可扩展性等方面的最新进展，展示了平台持续演进的成果

Conclusion: PySCF已成为电子结构理论研究和量子化学方法开发的重要平台，其持续发展将推动计算化学领域的创新

Abstract: Over the past decade, the Python-based Simulations of Chemistry Framework (PySCF) has developed into a widely used open-source platform for electronic structure theory and quantum chemical method development. This article reviews the major advances since the previous overview in 2020, covering new modules and methodology, infrastructure changes, and performance benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Auto-WHATMD : Automated Wasserstein-based High-dimensional feature extraction Analysis of Trajectories from Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14414)
*Sosuke Asano,Ikki Yasuda,Katsuhiro Endo,Yoshinori Hirano,Kenji Yasuoka*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出auto-WHATMD算法，利用最优传输距离比较高维分子动力学轨迹，通过模拟退火识别最能区分多个蛋白质系统的关键残基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学模拟中比较多个蛋白质系统（如不同配体或突变）并理解其效应是重要目标。传统方法依赖领域专家选择特征，可能引入主观假设，需要自动化方法来识别关键特征。

Method: 开发auto-WHATMD算法：1) 使用最优传输距离（Wasserstein距离）比较高维轨迹数据；2) 采用模拟退火算法识别最能区分多个系统的残基；3) 应用于溴结构域4与不同配体的蛋白质-配体系统。

Result: 成功识别出溴结构域4环区最具区分性的残基。即使仅选择少数残基，也能捕捉与配体结合亲和力的相关性，表明算法能有效优先选择信息量最大的残基。

Conclusion: auto-WHATMD方法可高效确定多个类似系统中的关键残基并设计特征，为分子动力学轨迹分析提供自动化、数据驱动的特征提取方案。

Abstract: Comparing multiple protein systems with variation such as different binding ligands or mutations, and understanding their effects is one of the objectives in molecular dynamics simulations. Representation of these systems by a few features enables quantitative comparison. However, because molecular dynamics simulation trajectories are high-dimensional spatiotemporal data, selection of key features relies on domain expertise, sometimes introducing arbitrary assumptions. Here, we present an approach that uses the optimal transport distance to compare high-dimensional trajectory data, and employs simulated annealing to identify the residues that best distinguish multiple systems. We term this algorithm auto-WHATMD (automated Wasserstein-based High-dimensional feature extraction Analysis for Trajectories of Molecular Dynamics). We applied auto-WHATMD to multiple protein-ligand systems of bromodomain 4 with different ligands, identifying the most discriminative residues in the loop region. Moreover, even a few selected residues were sufficient to capture the correlation with ligand-binding affinities, indicating that auto-WHATMD effectively prioritizes the most informative residues. Our approach can be used to efficiently determine key residues and design features for multiple analogous systems.

</details>


### [112] [Carbon black and hydrogen production from methane pyrolysis: measured and modeled insights from integrated gas and particle diagnostics in shock tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14314)
*Gibson Clark,Mohammad Adib,Chengze Li,Taylor M. Rault,Jesse W. Streicher,Enoch Dames,M. Reza Kholghy,Ronald K. Hanson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 甲烷热解实验与建模研究，结合激光吸收诊断、多波长消光和TEM分析，为碳黑和氢气生产的模型改进提供综合基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷热解是同时生产氢气和碳黑的有前景途径，但需要实验数据来约束气体化学、颗粒形成和形态演化的模型开发

Method: 在反射激波后条件下进行甲烷热解实验，使用激光吸收诊断测量CH4、C2H4和C2H2摩尔分数，多波长消光（633和1064 nm）监测颗粒形成和光学成熟度，结合TEM分析颗粒尺寸分布和纳米结构

Result: 模拟能准确再现小分子物种，但PAH预测存在较大差异；耦合气体-颗粒模拟能捕捉体积分数趋势，但在高温下低估诱导时间；TEM显示随着温度升高，初级颗粒尺寸减小而石墨纳米结构增加

Conclusion: 该研究为改进甲烷热解生产碳黑和氢气的模型提供了综合基准，强调了颗粒数量和尺寸之间的质量分配准确性对模型开发的重要性

Abstract: Methane (CH4) pyrolysis is a promising route to co-produce hydrogen (H2) and carbon black (CB) while avoiding emissions associated with steam-methane reforming and furnace black processes. Model development of pyrolytic CB synthesis requires experimental observations of concurrent gas chemistry, particulate formation, and morphology. This work presents a combined experimental and modeling study of CH4 pyrolysis behind reflected shock waves in 5% CH4/Argon mixtures at post-reflected shock temperatures (T5) of 1850-2450 K and P5 around 4.5 atm. Laser absorption diagnostics quantified CH4, C2H4, and C2H2 mole fractions, while multiwavelength extinction (633 and 1064 nm) resolved time-dependent particle formation and the temperature-dependent evolution of optical maturity. Simulations reproduce small-molecule speciation well, but large variations in predicted polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) persist among models. Coupled gas-particle simulations capture accurate volume fraction (fv) trends and the influence of gas dynamics but underpredict induction times at high T5. Samples collected at the shock tube endwall were analyzed by transmission electron microscopy (TEM) to quantify primary particle size distributions and nanostructure arrangement. Image segmentation and manual measurements showed reduced primary particle size growth (dp) with increasing T5, while graphitic nanostructure generally increased. This study provides an integrated benchmark for improving models of CB and H2 production from CH4 pyrolysis by constraining gas-phase kinetics, PAH-driven inception, particle dynamics, and particle maturity. The results highlight that accurate partitioning of mass between particle number and particle size is an important constraint for further model development.

</details>


### [113] [Explicit, Machine-Learned Two-Body Potentials for Molecular Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14466)
*Kham Lek Chaton,Eric D. Boittier,Mike Devereux,Markus Meuwly*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种新的混合机器学习/分子力学（ML/MM）对势，用于大型异质凝聚相系统，其中ML处理单体及短程二聚体相互作用，MM处理长程相互作用，通过切换距离实现平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发适用于大型异质凝聚相系统的高精度势能函数，结合ML的高精度和MM的计算效率，同时为未来加入多体效应校正奠定基础。

Method: 采用PhysNet ML方法描述单体和短程二聚体相互作用，经典MM力场描述切换距离外的成对相互作用；模型基于MP2二聚体和成对簇能量拟合；评估不同切换距离和MM方法（含/不含分布式电荷静电）的性能。

Result: 方法在二氯甲烷和丙酮测试系统中能准确描述成对参考数据，分子动力学模拟验证了其在小模型系统中的适用性，但揭示了成对方法在具有显著多体效应的凝聚相系统中的局限性。

Conclusion: 该混合ML/MM对势为大型异质凝聚相系统提供了可行的解决方案，展示了ML/MM结合的潜力，同时明确了未来需要加入通用多体校正以处理显著多体效应系统。

Abstract: A new pairwise hybrid machine-learning/molecular mechanics (ML/MM) potential is introduced that is conceived for application to large, heterogeneous condensed-phase systems. The PhysNet ML method describes monomers and short-range dimer interactions, while a classical MM force field describes pairwise interactions beyond a defined switching distance. Models are fitted to MP2 dimer and pairwise cluster energies, and the quality of each model is assessed at different switching distances and using MM approaches with and without detailed distributed charge electrostatics. The applicability of the approach to molecular dynamics simulations is demonstrated for a basic implementation applied to a small model system. Dichloromethane and acetone are used as test systems to demonstrate the accuracy of the approach in describing pairwise reference data, and also to highlight the limitations of the pairwise approach for systems that exhibit significant many-body effects in condensed phase, paving the way for the addition of a general many-body correction in future work.

</details>


### [114] [Stochastic Collision Theory of Magnetism in Radical Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14677)
*Yoshiaki Uchida,Ryohei Kishi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过量子主方程模型揭示了浓缩自由基溶液中随机分子碰撞如何产生宏观铁磁耦合的机制，挑战了传统理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机微观事件如何产生确定性宏观性质这一物理学基本问题，特别关注浓缩自由基溶液中随机分子碰撞如何决定系统磁性的机制。

Method: 开发了浓缩自由基溶液的量子主方程模型，分析分子碰撞对系统磁性的影响，重点关注一阶和二阶交换贡献的统计平均效应。

Result: 理论揭示：一阶交换贡献在碰撞平均后为零，而二阶项作为有效铁磁耦合保留，增强磁化强度。该模型成功捕捉了与传统理论偏离的实验观测趋势。

Conclusion: 该机制源于统计平均，可能适用于更广泛的软物质现象（如液晶），为微观随机性与宏观确定性之间的关联提供了新见解。

Abstract: How stochastic, microscopic events generate deterministic, macroscopic properties is a fundamental question in physics. We address this question by developing a quantum master equation model for concentrated radical solutions, where random molecular collisions govern the magnetic properties of the system. Our theory reveals a simple mechanism: the first-order exchange contribution averages to zero over collisions, while the second-order term survives as an effective ferromagnetic coupling that enhances magnetization. The model captures the experimentally observed trends in magnetic behavior that deviate from conventional theories. Because the mechanism arises from statistical averaging, it may apply to a broader class of soft matter phenomena, including liquid crystals.

</details>


### [115] [Design Space of Self--Consistent Electrostatic Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14700)
*William J. Baldwin,Ilyes Batatia,Martin Vondrák,Johannes T. Margraf,Gábor Csányi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个将静电效应纳入机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）的框架，通过将现有模型视为密度泛函理论（DFT）的粗粒度近似，揭示了不同方法的联系与局限性，并开发了更灵活的自洽静电MLIPs设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLIPs大多基于短程原子能量贡献的局部性假设，这限制了其在长程静电、电荷转移或诱导极化起关键作用的系统中的适用性。虽然已有扩展模型尝试纳入静电效应，但其基本假设和局限性尚未被充分理解。

Method: 提出一个理论框架，将现有静电MLIPs视为DFT的粗粒度近似，明确其近似假设并阐明学习量的物理意义。基于此形式化框架，识别出定义自洽静电MLIPs设计空间的关键设计选择，并使用MACE架构和共享电荷密度表示实现不同方法，进行可控比较。

Result: 在金属-水界面（探测导体和绝缘体静电响应对比）和二氧化硅中带电空位两个测试案例上评估不同模型。结果显示现有方法存在局限性，需要更具表达力的自洽模型来解决失效问题。

Conclusion: 通过DFT视角建立的框架澄清了静电MLIPs的近似假设和物理意义，揭示了不同模型间的联系与等效性。研究强调了现有方法的局限性，并展示了更具表达力的自洽模型对于处理复杂静电系统的必要性。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become widely used tools in atomistic simulations. For much of the history of this field, the most commonly employed architectures were based on short-ranged atomic energy contributions, and the assumption of locality still persists in many modern foundation models. While this approach has enabled efficient and accurate modelling for many use cases, it poses intrinsic limitations for systems where long-range electrostatics, charge transfer, or induced polarization play a central role. A growing body of work has proposed extensions that incorporate electrostatic effects, ranging from locally predicted atomic charges to self-consistent models. While these models have demonstrated success for specific examples, their underlying assumptions, and fundamental limitations are not yet well understood. In this work, we present a framework for treating electrostatics in MLIPs by viewing existing models as coarse-grained approximations to density functional theory (DFT). This perspective makes explicit the approximations involved, clarifies the physical meaning of the learned quantities, and reveals connections and equivalences between several previously proposed models. Using this formalism, we identify key design choices that define a broader design space of self-consistent electrostatic MLIPs. We implement salient points in this space using the MACE architecture and a shared representation of the charge density, enabling controlled comparisons between different approaches. Finally, we evaluate these models on two instructive test cases: metal-water interfaces, which probe the contrasting electrostatic response of conducting and insulating systems, and charged vacancies in silicon dioxide. Our results highlight the limitations of existing approaches and demonstrate how more expressive self-consistent models are needed to resolve failures.

</details>


### [116] [Reproducible Orchestration of Best Practices for Reaction Path Optimization with the Nudged Elastic Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14737)
*Rohit Goswami*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 开发了一个基于Snakemake的自动化工作流，用于小分子气相反应的NEB计算，集成了机器学习势函数和eOn软件，实现了从端点准备到路径优化的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NEB计算需要多个预处理步骤（端点最小化、结构对齐、初始路径生成），通常依赖临时脚本或手动操作，这容易引入错误且难以复现。需要开发一个完全自动化的工作流来解决这些问题。

Method: 开发了一个基于Snakemake的自动化工作流，集成了机器学习势函数（PET-MAD）和eOn鞍点搜索软件。工作流将计算生命周期的每个步骤编码为显式依赖图，包括模型检索、端点准备、路径初始化和能带优化。所有软件依赖都通过conda-forge解析，确保跨平台执行的一致性。

Result: 在HCN到HNC异构化反应上验证了自动化流程，成功恢复了已知的单势垒能量剖面和产物能量，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 该工作流实现了NEB计算的完全自动化，消除了传统方法中的手动步骤，提高了计算的可重复性和可靠性，特别适用于小分子气相反应的研究。

Abstract: The nudged elastic band (NEB) method is the standard approach for finding minimum energy paths and transition states on potential energy surfaces. Practical NEB calculations require several pre-processing steps: endpoint minimization, structural alignment, and initial path generation. These steps are typically handled by ad-hoc scripts or manual intervention, introducing errors and hindering reproducibility. We present a fully automated, open-source Snakemake workflow for small gas phase molecules that couples modern machine learning potentials (PET-MAD) to the eOn saddle point search software. Each step of the calculation lifecycle is encoded as an explicit dependency graph, from model retrieval and endpoint preparation through path initialization and band optimization. The workflow resolves all software dependencies from conda-forge, ensuring identical execution across platforms. Validation on the HCN to HNC isomerization demonstrates that the automated pipeline recovers the known single-barrier energy profile and product energy without manual intervention.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Optimality and annealing path planning of dynamical analog solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13778)
*Shu Zhou,K. Y. Michael Wong,Juntao Wang,David Shui Wing Hui,Daniel Ebler,Jie Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一个动力学平均场框架分析伊辛机求解Sherrington-Kirkpatrick模型基态能量的机制，证明可在常数时间内达到近最优解，并设计优化的参数调度方案提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动力学系统的模拟求解器（如伊辛机）在大规模组合优化中前景广阔，但该领域具有启发式性质，对求解器的最优性保证以及参数调度如何影响动力学和结果的理解非常有限。

Method: 开发动力学平均场框架分析伊辛机求解SK自旋玻璃模型基态能量的动力学过程，识别实现快速收敛到可证明近最优能量的机制，并研究不同参数调度轨迹的理论限制。

Result: 对于固定目标能量密度Ec，解通常在O(1)次矩阵向量乘法内达到，表明常数时间复杂度；发现仅温度退火对相干伊辛机有明显优势；设计出优化的参数调度框架，显著提升了伊辛机在复杂优化任务中的实际效果。

Conclusion: 动力学平均场框架为伊辛机提供了理论分析工具，揭示了实现快速收敛的机制，提出的参数调度优化方案显著提升了求解器性能，使伊辛机能够达到SK模型的基态能量，展示了动力学求解器在复杂优化中的优越性能。

Abstract: Recently proposed analog solvers based on dynamical systems, such as Ising machines, are promising platforms for large-scale combinatorial optimization. Yet, given the heuristic nature of the field, there is very limited insight on optimality guarantees of the solvers, as well as how parameter schedules shape dynamics and outcomes. Here, we develop a dynamical mean-field framework to analyze Ising-machine dynamics for finding the ground state energy of the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick(SK) model of spin glasses and identify mechanisms that enable rapid convergence to provenly near-optimal energies. For a fixed target energy density Ec, we show that solutions are typically reached within O(1) matrix vector multiplications, indicating constant time complexity. We further delineate theoretical limitations arising from different parameter-scheduling trajectories and demonstrate a pronounced benefit of temperature-only annealing for the Coherent Ising Machine. Building on these insights, we propose a general framework for designing optimized parameter schedules, thereby improving the practical effectiveness of Ising machines for complex optimization tasks. The superior performance of the dynamical solvers is illustrated by the attainment of the ground state energy of the SK model.

</details>


### [118] [Rigorous Asymptotics for First-Order Algorithms Through the Dynamical Cavity Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14573)
*Yatin Dandi,David Gamarnik,Francisco Pernice,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文形式化了动力学空腔方法，并用其为广义一阶方法推导出动力学平均场理论方程的新证明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学平均场理论在描述无序系统的宏观可观测量动力学方面有广泛应用，但物理学家使用的动力学空腔方法一直缺乏严格的数学形式化。现有数学形式化方法依赖于替代方法，需要建立动力学空腔方法的严格数学基础。

Method: 形式化动力学空腔方法，并将其应用于广义一阶方法（包括梯度下降和近似消息传递等算法）的动力学平均场理论方程推导。

Result: 成功形式化了动力学空腔方法，并给出了广义一阶方法动力学平均场理论方程的新证明，为这一重要物理工具提供了严格的数学基础。

Conclusion: 动力学空腔方法可以被严格形式化，并有效用于推导动力学平均场理论方程，为物理、高维统计和机器学习中的广泛模型提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) provides an asymptotic description of the dynamics of macroscopic observables in certain disordered systems. Originally pioneered in the context of spin glasses by Sompolinsky and Zippelius (1982), it has since been used to derive asymptotic dynamical equations for a wide range of models in physics, high-dimensional statistics and machine learning. One of the main tools used by physicists to obtain these equations is the dynamical cavity method, which has remained largely non-rigorous. In contrast, existing mathematical formalizations have relied on alternative approaches, including Gaussian conditioning, large deviations over paths, or Fourier analysis. In this work, we formalize the dynamical cavity method and use it to give a new proof of the DMFT equations for General First Order Methods, a broad class of dynamics encompassing algorithms such as Gradient Descent and Approximate Message Passing.

</details>


### [119] [Machine learning for sustainable geoenergy: uncertainty, physics and decision-ready inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14907)
*Hannah P. Menke,Ahmed H. Elsheikh,Lingli Wei,Nanzhe Wang,Andreas Busch*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文提出机器学习方法解决地热能源项目中四大瓶颈：数据稀缺、不确定性处理不足、尺度桥接薄弱、质量保证不足，应用于数字孪生、多相流、监测和盆地管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地热能源项目（CO2封存、地热、地下氢生成/储存等）面临从筛选到运营的复杂流程，需要在有限观测数据下做出风险可控的决策，这些决策影响部署速度、资本成本和气候减缓的可信度。当前进展受限于四大瓶颈：数据稀缺且偏倚、不确定性处理不足、尺度桥接薄弱、质量保证和治理不足。

Method: 提出匹配现实需求的机器学习方法：混合物理-ML模型、概率不确定性量化、结构感知表示、多保真度/持续学习。这些方法应用于四大核心应用：成像到过程的数字孪生、多相流和近井一致性、监测和反问题（包括变形和微震）、盆地尺度投资组合管理。

Result: 论文未提供具体实验结果，而是提出了一个方法论框架。该框架将机器学习技术与地热能源项目的实际需求相结合，为解决四大瓶颈提供了系统化的技术路径。

Conclusion: 需要制定实用的议程，包括基准测试、验证、报告标准、政策支持，以实现地热能源领域可重复且可辩护的机器学习应用。强调不确定性量化应作为核心交付成果，而非事后考虑。

Abstract: Geoenergy projects (CO2 storage, geothermal, subsurface H2 generation/storage, critical minerals from subsurface fluids, or nuclear waste disposal) increasingly follow a petroleum-style funnel from screening and appraisal to operations, monitoring, and stewardship. Across this funnel, limited and heterogeneous observations must be turned into risk-bounded operational choices under strong physical and geological constraints - choices that control deployment rate, cost of capital, and the credibility of climate-mitigation claims. These choices are inherently multi-objective, balancing performance against containment, pressure footprint, induced seismicity, energy/water intensity, and long-term stewardship. We argue that progress is limited by four recurring bottlenecks: (i) scarce, biased labels and few field performance outcomes; (ii) uncertainty treated as an afterthought rather than the deliverable; (iii) weak scale-bridging from pore to basin (including coupled chemical-flow-geomechanics); and (iv) insufficient quality assurance (QA), auditability, and governance for regulator-facing deployment. We outline machine learning (ML) approaches that match these realities (hybrid physics-ML, probabilistic uncertainty quantification (UQ), structure-aware representations, and multi-fidelity/continual learning) and connect them to four anchor applications: imaging-to-process digital twins, multiphase flow and near-well conformance, monitoring and inverse problems (monitoring, measurement, and verification (MMV), including deformation and microseismicity), and basin-scale portfolio management. We close with a pragmatic agenda for benchmarks, validation, reporting standards, and policy support needed for reproducible and defensible ML in sustainable geoenergy.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Terahertz cavity hybridization of collective proteins vibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14476)
*Elsa Perez-Martin,Laurent Bonnet,Songlin Fang,Jelle Bannink,Elwin Vrouwe,Cedric Bray,Frederic Teppe,Sandra Ruffenach,Elodie Strupiechonski,Zhedong Zhang,Jeremie Torres*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: R-藻红蛋白在室温下支持相干亚太赫兹振动模式，与太赫兹腔光子形成强集体耦合，为腔工程振动光谱学和生物传感开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现生物系统中驱动集体振动与光子的混合态是光子学领域的开放挑战，探索太赫兹腔作为稳定和控制集体分子振动动力学的平台。

Method: 使用光学泵浦的R-藻红蛋白在室温下，通过微流控腔平台将相干亚太赫兹振动模式与受限太赫兹腔光子杂交，进行定量光谱分析和微观非平衡分析。

Result: 观察到分辨的双峰结构、功率和浓度依赖的光谱权重重新分布、线宽变窄；耦合强度呈现集体平方根N标度，协作性和分裂-线宽比超过1，表明强集体耦合；Fröhlich极化子态弛豫时间约为1-10微秒。

Conclusion: 太赫兹腔可作为稳定和控制集体分子振动动力学的平台，为腔工程振动光谱学、无标记生物传感和复杂生物分子系统中能量传输的光子控制开辟新机遇。

Abstract: Hybrid light-matter states have transformed photonics, yet their realization with driven collective vibrations in biological systems remains an open challenge. Here we show that optically pumped R-phycoerythrin proteins at room temperature support coherent sub-terahertz vibrational modes consistent with Frohlich condensation, and that these modes hybridize with confined terahertz cavity photons in a microfluidic cavity platform. The resulting spectra exhibit a resolved doublet, power- and concentration-dependent redistribution of spectral weight, and linewidth narrowing indicative of cavity-modified dissipation. Quantitative analysis reveals collective square-root of N-scaling of the coupling strength, with cooperativity and splitting-to-linewidth ratios exceeding unity, consistent with the onset of strong collective coupling driven by the vibrational molecular mode. A microscopic nonequilibrium analysis further indicates that the relaxation timescale toward the Frohlich polariton state is on the order of 1-10 microseconds. These findings identify terahertz cavities as a platform for stabilizing and controlling collective molecular vibration dynamics and open opportunities for cavity-engineered vibrational spectroscopy, label-free biosensing and photonic control of energy transport in complex biomolecular systems.

</details>


### [121] [Demonstration of AI-Assisted Scientific Workflow on Canonical Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14888)
*Kin Hung Fung*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 论文展示了一个完全可复现的AI辅助科学工作流程，使用经典基准问题验证AI在推导、实现、验证、可视化和论文准备方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示当代AI如何作为科学研究的有效协作者，为技术研究实践提供一个可信赖的AI使用具体模板，而非追求新的科学发现。

Method: 采用完全可复现的工作流程，从单一用户提示生成初始项目工件栈，然后由人类作者审查和整理。使用具有精确解、制造解或可独立验证答案的经典基准问题，包括量子谐振子分析、热方程和泊松问题数值解、阻尼振荡数据反演建模、以及密集与稀疏求解器性能比较。

Result: AI能够成功辅助完成推导、实现、验证、可视化和论文准备等科学工作流程的各个阶段，前提是每个阶段都受到基准理论、显式验证和透明工件的约束。

Conclusion: 当代AI已经可以作为有用的科学协作者，但需要严格的验证框架和透明的工作流程来确保可信度。该演示为技术研究中可信赖的AI使用提供了具体模板。

Abstract: We present a fully reproducible demonstration of an AI-assisted scientific workflow designed for a broad physics, mathematics, and computer-science readership. The initial project artifact stack was generated from one single user prompt and then reviewed and curated for submission by the human author. Rather than claiming a new scientific discovery, the manuscript uses canonical benchmark problems with exact, manufactured, or independently checkable answers. The analytical component starts from the one-dimensional quantum harmonic oscillator, derives its dimensionless form, and validates finite-difference eigenpairs against exact Hermite-function benchmarks. The numerical partial-differential-equation component solves a heat equation with a known modal solution and a Poisson problem verified by a manufactured solution, with explicit convergence studies. The inverse-modeling component fits synthetic damped-oscillation data by nonlinear least squares and quantifies parametric uncertainty by bootstrap resampling. The computational-science component compares dense and sparse eigensolvers and contrasts direct and iterative sparse linear solvers, with careful interpretation of machine-dependent timing data. Taken together, the results show that contemporary AI can already serve as a useful scientific copilot for derivation, implementation, validation, visualization, and manuscript preparation, provided that each stage is constrained by benchmark theory, explicit verification, and transparent artifacts. The demonstration is therefore relevant not because the underlying science is novel, but because it offers a concrete template for trustworthy AI use in technical research practice.

</details>


### [122] [Measuring impurity-induced shifts in Coulomb crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14980)
*Mingyao Xu,Aaron A. Smith,Leonid Prokhorov,Vera Guarrera,Giovanni Barontini*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过激光冷却的Ca⁺晶体掺杂可控数量的Xe¹²⁺高电荷离子，测量杂质对强相互作用离子系统库仑结晶化的影响，发现低浓度时结晶阈值不变，高浓度时出现线性偏移，该全局效应源于杂质周围的局部钉扎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究杂质对强相互作用离子系统库仑结晶化的影响，为多组分库仑物质（如白矮星和中子星中的结晶过程）的建模提供实验基础，将杂质效应纳入天体物理和强耦合等离子体模型。

Method: 使用激光冷却的Ca⁺晶体，掺杂可控数量的Xe¹²⁺高电荷离子作为杂质，测量结晶阈值随杂质浓度的变化，并通过互补测量分析局部晶体结构。

Result: 低杂质浓度时结晶阈值保持不变，但一旦杂质含量足够高，结晶阈值出现明显交叉，随后偏移近似线性增长；该全局效应源于杂质周围晶体的局部钉扎现象。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了将杂质效应纳入多组分库仑物质模型的实验途径，对白矮星和中子星结晶的标准模型有重要影响，为强耦合等离子体和天体物理中的结晶过程研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We report a laboratory measurement of how impurities shift Coulomb crystallization in a strongly interacting ionic system. This is achieved by using laser cooled Ca$^+$ crystals doped with a controlled number of Xe$^{12+}$ highly charged ions. We find that the crystallization threshold is unchanged at low impurity concentration, but shows a clear crossover once the impurity content becomes sufficiently large, after which the shift grows approximately linearly. Complementary measurements reveal that this global effect originates from a local pinning of the crystal around the impurities. We further show how the measured shift could impact standard models of crystallization in white dwarfs and neutron stars. Our results provide an experimental route to incorporating impurity effects into models of multicomponent Coulomb matter, relevant to stellar crystallization and strongly coupled plasmas.

</details>
